  var PIXEL_FONT = "GoeiPixel";
  var img3 = (k) => `url("${KIT_IMG[k]}")`;
  var sl = (k) => KIT_META[k].slice;
  var px2 = (a) => a.map((v) => `${v * 2}px`).join(" ");
  function kitCss() {
    return `
@font-face { font-family: '${PIXEL_FONT}'; src: url('${PIXEL_FONT_URL}') format('woff2'); font-display: swap; }
.gk-font { font-family: '${PIXEL_FONT}', 'Galmuri11', monospace; -webkit-font-smoothing: none; font-smooth: never; }
.gk-panel { border-style: solid; border-width: ${px2(sl("panel"))}; border-image: ${img3("panel")} ${sl("panel").join(" ")} fill / ${px2(sl("panel"))} stretch; image-rendering: pixelated; background: none !important; border-radius: 0 !important; box-shadow: 0 10px 0 rgba(0,0,0,0.45) !important; }
.gk-btn { font-family: '${PIXEL_FONT}', monospace; -webkit-font-smoothing: none; font-size: 22px; font-weight: 700; color: #b8ae96; background: none; border: 0; border-style: solid; border-width: ${px2(sl("row"))}; border-color: transparent; border-image: none; border-radius: 0; text-shadow: 2px 2px 0 #000; cursor: pointer; image-rendering: pixelated; }
.gk-btn.on, .gk-btn.f, .gk-btn:focus-visible, .gk-btn:hover { color: #ffe27a; border-image: ${img3("row")} ${sl("row").join(" ")} fill / ${px2(sl("row"))} stretch; }
.gk-face { position: relative; image-rendering: pixelated; }
.gk-face > img { width: 100%; height: 100%; image-rendering: pixelated; display: block; }
.gk-face::after { content: ''; position: absolute; inset: -8px; border-style: solid; border-width: ${px2(sl("portrait"))}; border-image: ${img3("portrait")} ${sl("portrait").join(" ")} / ${px2(sl("portrait"))} stretch; pointer-events: none; image-rendering: pixelated; }
.gk-slot { border-style: solid; border-width: ${px2(sl("slot"))}; border-image: ${img3("slot")} ${sl("slot").join(" ")} fill / ${px2(sl("slot"))} stretch; image-rendering: pixelated; }
`;
  }
  var added = false;
  function ensureKit() {
    if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
    if (added && document.getElementById("goei-kit-css")) return;
    added = true;
    const st = document.createElement("style");
    st.id = "goei-kit-css";
    st.textContent = kitCss();
    document.head.append(st);
    try {
      const ff = new FontFace(PIXEL_FONT, `url(${PIXEL_FONT_URL})`);
      void ff.load().then((f) => document.fonts.add(f));
    } catch {
    }
  }
  var cache2 = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function kitImage(src) {
    if (typeof Image === "undefined") return null;
    let im = cache2.get(src);
    if (!im) {
      im = new Image();
      im.src = src;
      cache2.set(src, im);
    }
    return im.complete && im.naturalWidth ? im : null;
  }
  var kitPiece = (k) => kitImage(KIT_IMG[k]);
  var kitSlice = sl;
  function agentFaces(name) {
    return /쟈알디|jyaldi/i.test(name) ? FACE_JYALDI : null;
  }
  function nine(c, im, s, x, y, w, h, k = 2) {
    const [t, r, b, l] = s;
    const iw = im.naturalWidth, ih = im.naturalHeight;
    const T2 = t * k, R = r * k, B2 = b * k, L = l * k;
    const sx = [0, l, iw - r, iw], sy = [0, t, ih - b, ih];
    const dx = [x, x + L, x + w - R, x + w], dy = [y, y + T2, y + h - B2, y + h];
    c.save();
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++)
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const sw = sx[i + 1] - sx[i], sh = sy[j + 1] - sy[j], dw = dx[i + 1] - dx[i], dh = dy[j + 1] - dy[j];
        if (sw > 0 && sh > 0 && dw > 0 && dh > 0) c.drawImage(im, sx[i], sy[j], sw, sh, dx[i], dy[j], dw, dh);
      }
    c.restore();
  }

  // src/game/action/ui/pixelHud.ts
  var imgs = {};
  function img4(k) {
    let im = imgs[k];
    if (!im) {
      im = new Image();
      im.src = HUD_ART[k].src;
      imgs[k] = im;
    }
    return im.complete && im.naturalWidth ? im : null;
  }
  function pixelHudReady() {
    return ["slot", "item", "portrait", "hp", "gauge"].every((k) => img4(k));
  }
  var INK = "#e6e4dc";
  var DIM = "#5c6268";
  var EDGE = "#07090b";
  var HOLLOW = "#0f1216";
  var textCache = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function pixelText(s, color = INK, size2 = 11, edge = EDGE) {
    const key = `${s}|${color}|${size2}|${edge}`;
    const hit2 = textCache.get(key);
    if (hit2) return hit2;
    const f = `700 ${size2}px "${PIXEL_FONT}", monospace`;
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas");
    const g0 = cv.getContext("2d");
    g0.font = f;
    const tw = Math.ceil(g0.measureText(s).width);
    cv.width = Math.max(1, tw + 2);
    cv.height = size2 + 4;
    const g = cv.getContext("2d");
    g.font = f;
    g.textBaseline = "top";
    g.fillStyle = "#fff";
    g.fillText(s, 1, 1);
    const d3 = g.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height), px = d3.data;
    const [r, gg, b] = hex(color), [er, eg, eb] = edge ? hex(edge) : [0, 0, 0];
    const on = new Uint8Array(cv.width * cv.height);
    for (let i = 0; i < on.length; i++) on[i] = px[i * 4 + 3] > 110 ? 1 : 0;
    for (let y = 0; y < cv.height; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x++) {
      const i = y * cv.width + x, o = i * 4;
      if (on[i]) {
        px[o] = r;
        px[o + 1] = gg;
        px[o + 2] = b;
        px[o + 3] = 255;
        continue;
      }
      let near2 = false;
      if (edge) for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1 && !near2; dy++) for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
        const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
        if (nx >= 0 && ny >= 0 && nx < cv.width && ny < cv.height && on[ny * cv.width + nx]) {
          near2 = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (near2) {
        px[o] = er;
        px[o + 1] = eg;
        px[o + 2] = eb;
        px[o + 3] = 255;
      } else px[o + 3] = 0;
    }
    g.putImageData(d3, 0, 0);
    if (document.fonts?.check?.(f) !== false) textCache.set(key, cv);
    return cv;
  }
  function hex(c) {
    const n = parseInt(c.slice(1), 16);
    return [n >> 16 & 255, n >> 8 & 255, n & 255];
  }
  function put(c, t, x, y, align = "left") {
    const dx = align === "center" ? Math.round(x - t.width / 2) : align === "right" ? Math.round(x - t.width) : Math.round(x);
    c.drawImage(t, dx, Math.round(y));
  }
  var labelImgs = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  var labelCache = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function labelSprite(s, color) {
    const src = HUD_LABELS[s];
    if (!src) return null;
    const key = `${s}|${color}`, hit2 = labelCache.get(key);
    if (hit2) return hit2;
    let im = labelImgs.get(s);
    if (!im) {
      im = new Image();
      im.src = src;
      labelImgs.set(s, im);
    }
    if (!im.complete || !im.naturalWidth) return null;
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas");
    cv.width = im.naturalWidth + 2;
    cv.height = im.naturalHeight + 2;
    const g = cv.getContext("2d");
    const tint2 = (col) => {
      const t = document.createElement("canvas");
      t.width = im.naturalWidth;
      t.height = im.naturalHeight;
      const tg = t.getContext("2d");
      tg.drawImage(im, 0, 0);
      tg.globalCompositeOperation = "source-in";
      tg.fillStyle = col;
      tg.fillRect(0, 0, t.width, t.height);
      return t;
    };
    const edge = tint2(EDGE), body = tint2(color);
    for (const [dx, dy] of [[0, 1], [2, 1], [1, 0], [1, 2]]) g.drawImage(edge, dx, dy);
    g.drawImage(body, 1, 1);
    labelCache.set(key, cv);
    return cv;
  }
  function fillHollow(c, k, x, y, r, color) {
    const a = HUD_ART[k], [ix0, iy0, ix1, iy1] = a.inner;
    const lim = ix0 + Math.round((ix1 - ix0) * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, r)));
    a.rows.forEach((runs, row) => {
      if (!runs.length) return;
      c.fillStyle = color(row, iy0, iy1 - 1);
      for (const [s, e] of runs) {
        const ee = Math.min(e, lim);
        if (ee > s) c.fillRect(x + s, y + row, ee - s, 1);
      }
    });
  }
  function hpHue(r) {
    return Math.round(120 * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (r - 0.12) / 0.68)) / 10) * 10;
  }
  function drawPixelCard(c, s, x, y) {
    const P2 = HUD_ART.portrait, pim = img4("portrait"), him = img4("hp"), gim = img4("gauge");
    if (!pim || !him || !gim) return y;
    c.save();
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    x = Math.round(x);
    y = Math.round(y);
    const [fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1] = P2.inner, fw = fx1 - fx0, fh = fy1 - fy0;
    c.fillStyle = "rgba(12,15,19,0.6)";
    c.fillRect(x + fx0, y + fy0, fw, fh);
    if (s.face) c.drawImage(s.face, x + fx0, y + fy0, fw, fh);
    else put(c, pixelText(s.name.slice(0, 1) || "?", INK, 33), x + P2.w / 2, y + P2.h / 2 - 18, "center");
    if (s.hurtT > 0) {
      c.fillStyle = `rgba(255,40,40,${Math.min(0.6, s.hurtT * 1.6)})`;
      c.fillRect(x + fx0, y + fy0, fw, fh);
    }
    c.drawImage(pim, x, y);
    const bx = x + P2.w + 4;
    const nt = pixelText(s.name, INK, 22);
    put(c, nt, bx + 14, y + 6);
    if (s.armor) put(c, pixelText("◆".repeat(s.armor), "#cfd3d6", 11), bx + 20 + nt.width, y + 14);
    const hy = y + 34, r = Math.max(0, s.hp) / s.maxHp, low = r < 0.25;
    c.drawImage(him, bx, hy);
    fillHollow(c, "hp", bx, hy, s.trail / s.maxHp, () => "#d8d2c4");
    const hue = hpHue(r), blink = low && Math.sin(s.t * 10) > 0;
    fillHollow(c, "hp", bx, hy, r, (row, top, bot) => row === top ? `hsl(${hue},75%,${blink ? 80 : 68}%)` : row >= bot - 1 ? `hsl(${hue},70%,${blink ? 48 : 32}%)` : `hsl(${hue},68%,${blink ? 66 : 48}%)`);
    put(c, pixelText(`${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s.hp))} / ${Math.ceil(s.maxHp)}`, INK, 11), bx + HUD_ART.hp.inner[2], y + 18, "right");
    let end = Math.max(y + P2.h, hy + HUD_ART.hp.h + 14);
    if (s.gauge !== void 0) {
      const gy = hy + HUD_ART.hp.h + 5, full = s.gauge >= 100;
      const pulse = full ? 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(s.t * 7) : 0;
      const gx2 = bx + 16;
      c.drawImage(gim, gx2, gy);
      fillHollow(c, "gauge", gx2, gy, s.gauge / 100, (row, top) => full ? row === top ? "#fff6d0" : `rgb(255,${205 + 30 * pulse},${90 + 60 * pulse})` : row === top ? "#e8f4ff" : "#9fc6e0");
      if (full && s.superKey) put(c, pixelText(s.superKey, "#ffe9a0", 11), gx2 + HUD_ART.gauge.w + 4, gy - 3);
      end = Math.max(end, gy + HUD_ART.gauge.h);
    }
    c.restore();
    return end;
  }

  function icon(c, id, cx, cy, u, col) {
    const rows = ICONS[id];
    const x0 = Math.round(cx - 6 * u), y0 = Math.round(cy - 6 * u);
    c.fillStyle = "#000";
    rows.forEach((r, j) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) if (r[i] === "#") c.fillRect(x0 + i * u + u, y0 + j * u + u, u, u);
    });
    rows.forEach((r, j) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) if (r[i] !== ".") {
        c.fillStyle = r[i] === "o" ? HOLLOW : col;
        c.fillRect(x0 + i * u, y0 + j * u, u, u);
      }
    });
  }
  function drawPixelHotbar(c, slots, x, y, t) {
    const S2 = HUD_ART.slot, sim = img4("slot");
    if (!sim) return x;
    const gap = 4, [ix0, iy0, ix1, iy1] = S2.inner;
    c.save();
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    x = Math.round(x);
    y = Math.round(y);
    slots.forEach((sl2, i) => {
      const sx = x + i * (S2.w + gap), sy = y;
      const ready = sl2.cd <= 0 && !sl2.off;
      c.drawImage(sim, sx, sy);
      const iw = ix1 - ix0, ih = iy1 - iy0;
      if (sl2.active) {
        c.fillStyle = "#3a4249";
        c.fillRect(sx + ix0, sy + iy0, iw, ih);
      }
      icon(c, sl2.icon, sx + ix0 + iw / 2, sy + iy0 + ih / 2 - (sl2.bar !== void 0 ? 1 : 0), 2, ready ? sl2.full ? "#ffe9a0" : INK : DIM);
      if (sl2.cd > 0) {
        c.fillStyle = "rgba(4,6,8,0.7)";
        c.fillRect(sx + ix0, sy + iy0, iw, Math.round(ih * Math.min(1, sl2.cd)));
        if (sl2.cdText) put(c, pixelText(sl2.cdText, INK, 11), sx + ix0 + iw / 2, sy + iy0 + ih / 2 - 7, "center");
      }
      if (sl2.bar !== void 0) {
        const bw = iw - 4, v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, sl2.bar));
        c.fillStyle = "#000";
        c.fillRect(sx + ix0 + 2, sy + iy1 - 4, bw, 2);
        c.fillStyle = sl2.hot ? "#e8553a" : sl2.full ? "#ffe9a0" : INK;
        c.fillRect(sx + ix0 + 2, sy + iy1 - 4, Math.round(bw * v), 2);
      }
      if (sl2.full && Math.sin(t * 7) > 0) {
        c.strokeStyle = "#ffe9a0";
        c.lineWidth = 1;
        c.strokeRect(sx + ix0 + 0.5, sy + iy0 + 0.5, iw - 1, ih - 1);
      }
      const kt = pixelText(sl2.key, "#101317", 11, null);
      const kw = Math.max(11, kt.width + 2), kh = 13;
      c.fillStyle = EDGE;
      c.fillRect(sx + 1, sy + 1, kw + 2, kh + 2);
      c.fillStyle = ready ? INK : "#8a9096";
      c.fillRect(sx + 2, sy + 2, kw, kh);
      put(c, kt, sx + 2 + kw / 2, sy + 2, "center");
      if (sl2.badge) put(c, pixelText(sl2.badge, sl2.full ? "#ffe9a0" : INK, 11), sx + ix1 - 1, sy + iy1 - 17, "right");
      const col = ready ? INK : "#8a9096";
      put(c, labelSprite(sl2.name, col) ?? pixelText(sl2.name, col, 11), sx + S2.w / 2, sy + S2.h + 1, "center");
    });
    c.restore();
    return x + slots.length * (S2.w + gap) - gap;
  }
  var hotbarSize = (n) => ({ w: n * (HUD_ART.slot.w + 4) - 4, h: HUD_ART.slot.h + 18 });
  function drawPixelItems(c, items, x, y, keys2, flash, icon2) {
    const S2 = HUD_ART.item, im = img4("item");
    if (!im) return y;
    const [ix0, iy0, ix1, iy1] = S2.inner, iw = ix1 - ix0, ih = iy1 - iy0, gap = 4;
    c.save();
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    x = Math.round(x);
    y = Math.round(y);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const it = items[i] ?? { label: "", count: 0 }, sx = x + i * (S2.w + gap), dim2 = it.count <= 0;
      c.drawImage(im, sx, y);
      if ((flash[i] ?? 0) > 0) {
        c.fillStyle = `rgba(255,240,180,${flash[i]})`;
        c.fillRect(sx + ix0, y + iy0, iw, ih);
      }
      if (it.label || it.id) {
        c.save();
        c.globalAlpha = dim2 ? 0.3 : 1;
        icon2(it, sx + ix0 + 1, y + iy0 + 1, Math.min(iw, ih) - 2);
        c.restore();
        put(c, pixelText(`${it.count}`, dim2 ? "#6a7276" : INK, 11), sx + ix1 - 1, y + iy1 - 13, "right");
      }
      if (it.cd && it.cd > 0) {
        c.fillStyle = "rgba(4,6,8,0.6)";
        c.fillRect(sx + ix0, y + iy0, iw, Math.round(ih * Math.min(1, it.cd)));
      }
      if (keys2[i]) {
        const kt = pixelText(keys2[i], "#101317", 11, null), kw = Math.max(11, kt.width + 2), kh = 13;
        c.fillStyle = EDGE;
        c.fillRect(sx + 1, y + 1, kw + 2, kh + 2);
        c.fillStyle = INK;
        c.fillRect(sx + 2, y + 2, kw, kh);
        put(c, kt, sx + 2 + kw / 2, y + 2, "center");
      }
    }
    c.restore();
    return y + S2.h;
  }
  function drawGuardBar(c, x, y, v, broken, a, t) {
    if (a <= 0.01) return;
    const W5 = 56, H5 = 8;
    const x0 = Math.round(x - W5 / 2), y0 = Math.round(y);
    c.save();
    c.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, a);
    const sh = ["#####", "#####", "#####", ".###.", "..#.."];
    const col = broken ? Math.sin(t * 14) > 0 ? "#ff5a44" : "#7a2a22" : INK;
    c.fillStyle = EDGE;
    c.fillRect(x0 - 10, y0 - 1, 9, 9);
    sh.forEach((row, j) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) if (row[i] === "#") {
        c.fillStyle = col;
        c.fillRect(x0 - 8 + i, y0 + 1 + j, 1, 1);
      }
    });
    c.fillStyle = EDGE;
    c.fillRect(x0 - 1, y0 - 1, W5 + 2, H5 + 2);
    c.fillStyle = "#2e353c";
    c.fillRect(x0, y0, W5, H5);
    c.fillStyle = "#151a1f";
    c.fillRect(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, W5 - 2, H5 - 2);
    const fw = Math.round((W5 - 2) * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v)));
    if (fw > 0) {
      c.fillStyle = broken ? "#c8483a" : v < 0.3 ? "#e8b04a" : "#cfe4f2";
      c.fillRect(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, fw, H5 - 2);
      c.fillStyle = broken ? "#ff8a76" : "#ffffff";
      c.fillRect(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, fw, 1);
    }
    if (broken) {
      c.fillStyle = INK;
      c.fillRect(x0 + 1 + Math.round((W5 - 2) * 0.4), y0, 1, H5);
    }
    c.restore();
  }

/* 가로 화면 띠 단추: 도트 금속 칸 + 도트 글꼴, 키 이름 없음 (검수 12) */
/* GPT 도트 단추 판 (tools/ui_pixelize.py): 판 그림이 테두리·그림을 다 갖고 있다 */
/* 아이템 칸: 판을 단추 상자 전체에 맞춰 늘려 수량 숫자가 판 안에 들어오게 (독립 검수: 수량이 칸 밖) */
`;

  // src/game/action/ui/crt.ts
  var VS = `attribute vec2 p; varying vec2 uv; void main(){ uv = p * 0.5 + 0.5; uv.y = 1.0 - uv.y; gl_Position = vec4(p, 0.0, 1.0); }`;
  var FS = `precision mediump float;
varying vec2 uv;
uniform sampler2D tex; uniform sampler2D prev;
uniform vec2 res; uniform vec2 src; uniform float time; uniform float bend;
vec2 curve(vec2 u){ if (bend < 0.5) return u; u = u * 2.0 - 1.0; vec2 o = abs(u.yx) / vec2(5.2, 4.2); u = u + u * o * o; return u * 0.5 + 0.5; }
vec3 sampleRGB(vec2 u, float ca){
  vec2 d = (u - 0.5) * ca;
  return vec3(texture2D(tex, u + d).r, texture2D(tex, u).g, texture2D(tex, u - d).b);
}
void main(){
  vec2 u = curve(uv);
  if (u.x < 0.0 || u.x > 1.0 || u.y < 0.0 || u.y > 1.0) { gl_FragColor = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0); return; }
  // 둥근 모서리 (곡면 모드만)
  vec2 q = abs(u - 0.5) * 2.0; float corner = bend < 0.5 ? 1.0 : smoothstep(1.0, 0.985, length(max(q - vec2(0.93, 0.9), 0.0) / vec2(0.07, 0.1)));
  float edge = dot(u - 0.5, u - 0.5);
  vec3 col = sampleRGB(u, 0.0012 + edge * 0.006);
  // 빛 번짐: 둘레 8곳을 흐리게 모아 밝은 곳만 더한다
  vec2 px = 1.0 / src; vec3 b = vec3(0.0);
  for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) { float a = float(i) * 0.785398; b += texture2D(tex, u + vec2(cos(a), sin(a)) * px * 2.5).rgb; }
  b /= 8.0; col += max(b - 0.55, 0.0) * 0.9;
  // 주사선: 원본 한 줄마다, 밝을수록 굵게(어두운 틈이 좁아짐)
  float lum = dot(col, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
  float line = 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(u.y * src.y * 3.14159265); // 원본 두 줄마다 한 번 (물결무늬 없게)
  float scan = mix(1.0, mix(0.62, 0.9, lum), line);
  col *= scan;
  // RGB 형광 마스크 (화면 픽셀 세 칸마다 빨강·초록·파랑 세로줄)
  float m = mod(gl_FragCoord.x, 3.0);
  vec3 mask = m < 1.0 ? vec3(1.0, 0.72, 0.72) : m < 2.0 ? vec3(0.72, 1.0, 0.72) : vec3(0.72, 0.72, 1.0);
  col *= mix(vec3(1.0), mask, 0.32);
  // 비네팅 · 밝기 보정
  col *= 1.0 - edge * (bend < 0.5 ? 0.35 : 0.95);
  // 깜빡임·움직이는 잡음은 뺐다 (사용자 2026-10-25: 눈 아픔)
  col = pow(col * 1.22, vec3(0.95));
  gl_FragColor = vec4(col * corner, 1.0);
}`;
  function mountCrt(stageEl, canvas) {
    const out = document.createElement("canvas");
    out.className = "gs-crtgl";
    out.style.cssText = "position:absolute;left:0;top:0;pointer-events:none;display:none";
    const gl = out.getContext("webgl", { antialias: false, alpha: false, preserveDrawingBuffer: false });
    if (!gl) return null;
    const sh = (type, srcText) => {
      const s = gl.createShader(type);
      gl.shaderSource(s, srcText);
      gl.compileShader(s);
      return s;
    };
    const prog = gl.createProgram();
    gl.attachShader(prog, sh(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VS));
    gl.attachShader(prog, sh(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FS));
    gl.linkProgram(prog);
    if (!gl.getProgramParameter(prog, gl.LINK_STATUS)) return null;
    gl.useProgram(prog);
    const buf = gl.createBuffer();
    gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buf);
    gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array([-1, -1, 1, -1, -1, 1, 1, 1]), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
    const loc = gl.getAttribLocation(prog, "p");
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(loc);
    gl.vertexAttribPointer(loc, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
    const mkTex = (unit) => {
      const t = gl.createTexture();
      gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0 + unit);
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, t);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, 1, 1, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, new Uint8Array([0, 0, 0, 255]));
      return t;
    };
    const tex = mkTex(0);
    mkTex(1);
    gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "tex"), 0);
    gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "prev"), 1);
    const uRes = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "res"), uSrc = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "src"), uTime = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "time"), uBend = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "bend");
    stageEl.insertBefore(out, canvas.nextSibling);
    let on = false, raf = 0, bend = 1;
    const frame = (t) => {
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      const cw = canvas.clientWidth || canvas.width, ch = canvas.clientHeight || canvas.height;
      const scale = stageEl.getBoundingClientRect().width / (stageEl.offsetWidth || 1) * (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
      const W5 = Math.max(1, Math.round(cw * scale)), H5 = Math.max(1, Math.round(ch * scale));
      if (out.width !== W5 || out.height !== H5) {
        out.width = W5;
        out.height = H5;
      }
      out.style.width = `${cw}px`;
      out.style.height = `${ch}px`;
      gl.viewport(0, 0, W5, H5);
      gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, canvas);
      gl.uniform2f(uRes, W5, H5);
      gl.uniform2f(uSrc, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.uniform1f(uTime, t / 1e3);
      gl.uniform1f(uBend, bend);
      gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLE_STRIP, 0, 4);
    };
    return {
      setOn(v, curved = true) {
        bend = curved ? 1 : 0;
        if (v === on) return;
        on = v;
        out.style.display = v ? "block" : "none";
        if (v) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        else cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      },
      dispose() {
        cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
        out.remove();
      }
    };
  }

  // src/game/action/ui/stage.ts
  var W = 1280;
  var H = 720;
  var portraitTop = (bh, h) => Math.max(8, Math.min((bh - h) * 0.35, bh - h));
  var cssAdded = false;
  function mountStage(box2) {
    if (!cssAdded || !document.getElementById("goei-ui-css")) {
      const st = document.createElement("style");
      st.id = "goei-ui-css";
      st.textContent = ui_default;
      document.head.append(st);
      cssAdded = true;
    }
    ensureKit();
    const root = document.createElement("div");
    root.className = "gs";
    root.tabIndex = 0;
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="gs-stage"><canvas class="gs-canvas" width="${W}" height="${H}" aria-label="게임 화면"></canvas><div class="gs-crt"></div><div class="gs-ui"></div></div><div class="gs-touch"></div><div class="gs-rotate"><div class="gs-rot-ico"></div><b>폰을 가로로 돌려 주세요</b><span>세로 그대로면 화면이 아주 작습니다</span><div class="gs-rot-row"><button type="button" data-rot="turn">화면 돌려서 크게</button><button type="button" data-rot="keep">세로로 작게</button></div></div>`;
    box2.append(root);
    const stageEl = root.querySelector(".gs-stage");
    const canvas = root.querySelector("canvas");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { alpha: false });
    const ui = root.querySelector(".gs-ui");
    const touch = root.querySelector(".gs-touch");
    const rotate = root.querySelector(".gs-rotate");
    const fns = [];
    let crt;
    let keepPortrait = false;
    let fill = false, byHeight = false;
    const coarse = () => typeof matchMedia !== "undefined" && matchMedia("(pointer: coarse)").matches;
    let turn = coarse();
    const s = {
      root,
      stage: stageEl,
      canvas,
      ctx,
      ui,
      touch,
      scale: 1,
      vw: W,
      setFit(mode) {
        fill = mode === "fill";
        byHeight = mode === "height";
        layout();
      },
      portrait: false,
      turned: false,
      toLocal(cx, cy, el) {
        if (!s.turned) {
          const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
          return { x: cx - r.left, y: cy - r.top };
        }
        const b = box2.getBoundingClientRect();
        let x = cy - b.top, y = b.width - (cx - b.left);
        for (let n = el; n && n !== root; n = n.offsetParent) x -= n.offsetLeft, y -= n.offsetTop;
        return { x, y };
      },
      toGame(cx, cy) {
        const p = s.toLocal(cx, cy, root);
        const m = /translate\(([-\d.]+)px, ([-\d.]+)px\)/.exec(stageEl.style.transform);
        return { x: (p.x - (m ? +m[1] : 0)) / s.scale, y: (p.y - (m ? +m[2] : 0)) / s.scale };
      },
      onResize(fn2) {
        fns.push(fn2);
      },
      setCrt(mode) {
        const on = mode !== "off";
        crt ??= mountCrt(stageEl, canvas);
        if (crt) crt.setOn(on, mode === "curved");
        root.classList.toggle("crt", on && !crt);
        root.dataset.crt = on ? mode : "off";
      },
      setBright(v) {
        canvas.style.filter = Math.abs(v - 1) < 0.01 ? "" : `brightness(${v})`;
      },
      setPixel(on) {
        root.classList.toggle("pixel", on);
      },
      async fullscreen() {
        try {
          if (!document.fullscreenElement) await (root.requestFullscreen?.({ navigationUI: "hide" }) ?? Promise.resolve());
          await screen.orientation.lock?.("landscape");
        } catch {
        }
        layout();
      },
      dispose() {
        ro.disconnect();
        crt?.dispose();
        crt = null;
        root.remove();
      }
    };
    const layout = () => {
      const ow = box2.clientWidth, oh = box2.clientHeight;
      const turnNow = turn && oh > ow * 1.05;
      if (turnNow !== s.turned || turnNow) {
        s.turned = turnNow;
        root.classList.toggle("turned", turnNow);
        Object.assign(root.style, turnNow ? { position: "absolute", left: "0", top: "0", width: `${oh}px`, height: `${ow}px`, transformOrigin: "0 0", transform: `translate(${ow}px, 0) rotate(90deg)` } : { position: "", left: "", top: "", width: "", height: "", transformOrigin: "", transform: "" });
      }
      const bw = root.clientWidth, bh = root.clientHeight;
      if (!bw || !bh) return;
      const portrait = bh > bw * 1.05;
      s.portrait = portrait;
      root.classList.toggle("portrait", portrait);
      const dpr2 = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      const fieldFill = byHeight && !portrait && bh >= 600;
      const k = fieldFill ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(dpr2)) / dpr2 : 1;
      const vh = fieldFill ? Math.round(bh / k) : H;
      const vw = fieldFill ? Math.round(bw / k) : (fill || byHeight) && !portrait && bw / bh > W / H ? Math.min(byHeight ? 2e3 : 1800, Math.round(H * bw / bh)) : byHeight && !portrait && bw / bh < W / H ? Math.max(640, Math.round(H * bw / bh)) : W;
      if (vw !== s.vw || canvas.width !== vw || canvas.height !== vh) {
        s.vw = vw;
        canvas.width = vw;
        canvas.height = vh;
        canvas.style.width = stageEl.style.width = `${vw}px`;
        canvas.style.height = stageEl.style.height = `${vh}px`;
      }
      const scale = fieldFill ? k : Math.min(bw / vw, bh / H);
      s.scale = scale;
      const w = s.vw * scale, h = vh * scale;
      const top = portrait ? portraitTop(bh, h) : (bh - h) / 2;
      stageEl.style.transform = `translate(${Math.round((bw - w) / 2)}px, ${Math.round(top)}px) scale(${scale})`;
      root.style.setProperty("--gs-scale", String(scale));
      root.style.setProperty("--gs-top", `${top}px`);
      root.style.setProperty("--gs-bottom", `${top + h}px`);
      rotate.classList.toggle("on", portrait && coarse() && !keepPortrait);
      for (const f of fns) f();
    };
    rotate.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest("[data-rot]");
      if (!b) return;
      keepPortrait = true;
      if (b.dataset.rot === "turn") turn = true;
      layout();
    });
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(layout);
    ro.observe(root);
    ro.observe(box2);
    layout();
    return s;
  }

  // src/game/action/ui/mapLayout.ts
  function setExplorationMap(canvas, open) {
    canvas.closest(".gs")?.classList.toggle("exploration-map", open);
  }

  // src/game/action/engine/hint.ts
  var SHAPE = [
    "..###..",
    "..#o#..",
    "..#o#..",
    "###o###",
    "#ooooo#",
    ".#ooo#.",
    "..#o#..",
    "...#..."
  ];
  var D = 4;
  function drawHintIcon(c, x, y, tick, state2 = "available") {
    if (state2 === "decoration") return;
    const bob = Math.round(Math.sin(tick / 5) * 1.5) * D;
    const w = SHAPE[0].length * D;
    const ox = Math.round(x - w / 2);
    const oy = Math.round(y - SHAPE.length * D + bob);
    c.save();
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    const P2 = 4;
    const R = 9;
    const cx = Math.round(x), cy = Math.round(oy + SHAPE.length * D / 2);
    for (let gy = -R; gy <= R; gy++)
      for (let gx = -R; gx <= R; gx++) {
        const d3 = Math.hypot(gx, gy);
        if (d3 > R + 0.3) continue;
        c.fillStyle = d3 > R - 1.2 ? state2 === "locked" ? "#c87858" : "#c89a3a" : d3 > R - 2.2 ? "#1a1410" : "rgba(20,16,12,0.82)";
        c.fillRect(cx + gx * P2 - P2 / 2, cy + gy * P2 - P2 / 2, P2, P2);
      }
    if (state2 === "locked") {
      c.font = "bold 28px sans-serif";
      c.textAlign = "center";
      c.textBaseline = "middle";
      c.fillStyle = "#ffd1ae";
      c.fillText("!", cx, cy);
    } else for (let r = 0; r < SHAPE.length; r++)
      for (let k = 0; k < SHAPE[r].length; k++) {
        const ch = SHAPE[r][k];
        if (ch === ".") continue;
        c.fillStyle = ch === "#" ? "#1a1410" : r < 3 ? "#fff4c8" : "#f0c860";
        c.fillRect(ox + k * D, oy + r * D, D, D);
      }
    c.restore();
  }

  // src/game/action/ui/hud.ts
  var W2 = W;
  var H2 = H;
  var FONT = '"Pretendard","Noto Sans KR","Apple SD Gothic Neo","Malgun Gothic",sans-serif';
  var C = {
    plate: "rgba(7,11,15,0.84)",
    edge: "rgba(200,220,230,0.16)",
    text: "#ece6d4",
    dim: "#9aa6ab",
    hp: "#f6d23a",
    hpLow: "#ff3a2a",
    trail: "#e8402c",
    hpMid: "#f2a62c",
    frame: "#e8dcb8",
    name: "#ffe9a8",
    energy: "#4aa8e8",
    gauge: "#f2b632",
    gaugeFill: "#3ec8f0",
    favorUp: "#f2c94c",
    favorDown: "#d8443c",
    rule: "#a9e0a0",
    amber: "#f0b45a"
  };
  function font(size2, weight = 700) {
    return pixelOn ? pfont2(size2).f : `${weight} ${size2}px ${FONT}`;
  }
  function pfont2(size2) {
    const n = size2 <= 12 ? 11 : Math.max(22, Math.round(size2 / 11) * 11);
    return { n, f: `700 ${n}px "${PIXEL_FONT}", ${FONT}` };
  }
  function drawPerkChips(c, list, right, left = 360) {
    const gap = 4, avail = right - left, perRow = Math.ceil(list.length / 2);
    const sz = Math.max(12, Math.min(26, Math.floor((avail + gap) / Math.max(1, perRow)) - gap));
    const cols = Math.max(1, Math.floor((avail + gap) / (sz + gap)));
    const small = sz < 22, top = 14;
    c.save();
    list.forEach((p, i) => {
      const col = i % cols, row = Math.floor(i / cols);
      if (row > 1) return;
      const x = Math.round(right - (col + 1) * sz - col * gap), y = top + row * (sz + gap + 4);
      c.fillStyle = "#000";
      c.fillRect(x - 1, y - 1, sz + 2, sz + 2);
      c.fillStyle = p.c;
      c.fillRect(x, y, sz, sz);
      c.fillStyle = "#1b1d22";
      c.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, sz - 4, sz - 4);
      label(c, small ? p.t.slice(0, 1) : p.t, x + sz / 2, y + sz / 2 + 4, small ? 10 : 11, "#fff", "center", 800);
      if (p.n) label(c, String(p.n), x + sz + 2, y + sz + 4, 10, "#ffe08a", "right", 900);
    });
    c.restore();
  }
  function label(c, s, x, y, size2 = 16, color = C.text, align = "left", weight = 700) {
    const p = pixelOn ? pfont2(size2) : null;
    if (p) size2 = p.n;
    c.font = p ? p.f : font(size2, weight);
    c.textAlign = align;
    c.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    c.lineJoin = p ? "miter" : "round";
    c.lineWidth = p ? Math.max(3, size2 / 5.5) : Math.max(3, size2 / 4.5);
    c.strokeStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)";
    c.strokeText(s, x, y);
    c.fillStyle = color;
    c.fillText(s, x, y);
  }
  var pixelOn = false;
  function fitText(c, text, width) {
    if (c.measureText(text).width <= width) return text;
    const chars2 = Array.from(text);
    while (chars2.length && c.measureText(chars2.join("") + "…").width > width) chars2.pop();
    return chars2.join("") + "…";
  }
  function wrapText(c, text, width, maxRows) {
    const rows = [];
    let line2 = "";
    const chars2 = Array.from(text.replace(/\s+/g, " "));
    for (let i = 0; i < chars2.length; i++) {
      if (line2 && c.measureText(line2 + chars2[i]).width > width) {
        rows.push(line2);
        if (rows.length === maxRows - 1) return [...rows, fitText(c, chars2.slice(i).join(""), width)];
        line2 = "";
      }
      line2 += chars2[i];
    }
    if (line2) rows.push(line2);
    return rows;
  }
  function setPixelText(on) {
    pixelOn = on;
  }
  function statusChips(c, list, x, y) {
    let cx = x;
    for (const st of list) {
      c.font = font(13, 800);
      const w = c.measureText(st.label).width + 14;
      c.beginPath();
      c.roundRect(cx, y, w, 22, 11);
      c.fillStyle = st.good ? "rgba(60,140,90,0.9)" : "rgba(150,50,50,0.9)";
      c.fill();
      label(c, st.label, cx + 7, y + 16, 13, "#fff", "left", 800);
      cx += w + 6;
    }
  }
  function plate2(c, x, y, w, h, r = 8) {
    c.beginPath();
    c.roundRect(x, y, w, h, r);
    c.fillStyle = C.plate;
    c.fill();
    c.strokeStyle = C.edge;
    c.lineWidth = 1;
    c.stroke();
  }
  function bar(c, x, y, w, h, v, color, back = "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)") {
    c.fillStyle = back;
    c.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    c.fillStyle = color;
    c.fillRect(x, y, Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v)) * w, h);
  }
  var Hud = class {
    trail = -1;
    trailHold = 0;
    lastHp = -1;
    hurtT = 0;
    toasts = [];
    t = 0;
    lastFloor = "";
    itemFlash = [0, 0, 0, 0];
    /** 마지막으로 그린 상태 (일시정지 메뉴가 재화·총애·규칙을 보여 줄 때 쓴다) */
    last = null;
    lastItems = [];
    toast(text, color = C.text) {
      this.toasts.push({ text, color, t: 0 });
      if (this.toasts.length > 5) this.toasts.shift();
    }
    reset() {
      this.trail = -1;
      this.lastHp = -1;
      this.lastItems = [];
    }
    draw(c, s, dt) {
      setExplorationMap(c.canvas, false);
      this.t += dt;
      this.track(s, dt);
      c.save();
      const k = s.scale ?? (s.compact ? 1.2 : 1);
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      H2 = c.canvas.height / k;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      if (s.prompt) s = { ...s, prompt: { ...s.prompt, x: s.prompt.x / k, y: s.prompt.y / k } };
      this.last = s;
      pixelOn = true;
      this.agent(c, s);
      this.floor(c, s);
      if (!s.compact && !(s.agent.portrait && pixelHudReady())) this.items(c, s.items, s.device);
      if (s.boss) this.boss(c, s.boss, s.compact);
      this.timer(c, s);
      if (s.message) this.message(c, s.message, s.compact, (s.messageLeft ?? 0) / k);
      this.navigation(c, s);
      this.drawToasts(c, dt);
      pixelOn = false;
      c.restore();
    }
    track(s, dt) {
      if (this.lastHp < 0 || this.trail < 0) this.trail = s.hp;
      if (this.lastHp >= 0 && s.hp < this.lastHp) this.hurtT = 0.35, this.trailHold = 0.5;
      this.lastHp = s.hp;
      if (this.trail < s.hp) this.trail = s.hp;
      else if (this.trailHold > 0) this.trailHold -= dt;
      else this.trail = Math.max(s.hp, this.trail - s.maxHp * dt * 0.6);
      this.hurtT = Math.max(0, this.hurtT - dt);
      s.items.forEach((it, i) => {
        if (this.lastItems[i] !== void 0 && it.count !== this.lastItems[i]) this.itemFlash[i] = 0.5;
        this.lastItems[i] = it.count;
        this.itemFlash[i] = Math.max(0, this.itemFlash[i] - dt);
      });
      const fk = s.floor.depth + s.floor.name;
      if (fk !== this.lastFloor) this.lastFloor = fk;
    }
    /** 왼쪽 위 한 묶음: 얼굴 + 이름 + 굵은 체력 막대 + 필살 게이지 + 탄. 판 없이 오락실처럼 */
    agent(c, s) {
      const x = s.compact ? 100 : 22, y = 18, fs = 80;
      if (s.agent.portrait && pixelHudReady()) {
        const end = drawPixelCard(c, { name: s.agent.name, face: s.agent.portrait, hp: s.hp, maxHp: s.maxHp, trail: this.trail, hurtT: this.hurtT, gauge: s.gauge, superKey: s.device === "pad" ? "RT" : s.device === "touch" ? "" : superKey(), armor: s.armor, t: this.t }, x - 6, y - 4);
        const iy = s.compact ? end : this.pixelItems(c, s.items, s.device, x + 78, end + 6);
        if (s.weapon) this.ammo(c, s.weapon, x + 90, iy + 26);
        this.meterRows(c, s, x, Math.max(y + fs + 52, iy + (s.weapon ? 40 : 10)));
        return;
      }
      const g = c.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 460, 0);
      g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0.5)");
      g.addColorStop(0.75, "rgba(0,0,0,0.25)");
      g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
      c.fillStyle = g;
      c.fillRect(0, 0, 460, y + fs + 30);
      const r0 = Math.max(0, s.hp) / s.maxHp;
      const faces2 = s.agent.portrait ? null : agentFaces(s.agent.name);
      const face = faces2 ? kitImage(faces2[this.hurtT > 0 ? 1 : r0 < 0.25 ? 2 : s.gauge >= 100 ? 3 : 0]) : null;
      c.fillStyle = s.agent.color ?? "#5a6a74";
      if (face || s.agent.portrait) c.fillStyle = "#141b20";
      c.fillRect(x, y, fs, fs);
      c.save();
      c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      if (s.agent.portrait) c.drawImage(s.agent.portrait, x, y, fs, fs);
      else if (face) c.drawImage(face, x, y, fs, fs);
      else label(c, s.agent.name.slice(0, 1), x + fs / 2, y + fs * 0.72, 44, "#fff", "center", 900);
      c.restore();
      if (this.hurtT > 0) c.fillStyle = `rgba(255,40,40,${this.hurtT * 1.2})`, c.fillRect(x, y, fs, fs);
      const pf = kitPiece("portrait");
      if (pf) nine(c, pf, kitSlice("portrait"), x - 12, y - 12, fs + 24, fs + 24, 2);
      else {
        c.strokeStyle = C.frame;
        c.lineWidth = 3;
        c.strokeRect(x - 1.5, y - 1.5, fs + 3, fs + 3);
      }
      if (s.maxEnergy) bar(c, x - 2, y + fs + 12, fs + 4, 5, (s.energy ?? 0) / s.maxEnergy, "#e8e2d0", "#000");
      const bx = x + fs + 30, bw = s.compact ? 250 : 300;
      label(c, s.agent.name, bx, y + 20, 22, C.name, "left", 900);
      c.font = font(22, 900);
      let ax = bx + c.measureText(s.agent.name).width + 10;
      if (s.armor) label(c, "◆".repeat(s.armor), ax, y + 19, 15, C.amber), ax += 50;
      const by = y + 30, bh = 24;
      const r = r0;
      const low = r < 0.25;
      c.fillStyle = "#000";
      c.fillRect(bx - 3, by - 3, bw + 6, bh + 6);
      c.fillStyle = "#3a1410";
      c.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
      c.fillStyle = C.trail;
      c.fillRect(bx, by, bw * this.trail / s.maxHp, bh);
      const hc = low ? Math.sin(this.t * 10) > 0 ? C.hpLow : C.hp : r < 0.5 ? C.hpMid : C.hp;
      c.fillStyle = hc;
      c.fillRect(bx, by, bw * r, bh);
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.35)";
      c.fillRect(bx, by + 3, bw * r, 4);
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.25)";
      c.fillRect(bx, by + bh - 5, bw * r, 5);
      const bf = kitPiece("bar");
      if (bf) nine(c, bf, kitSlice("bar"), bx - 22, by - 10, bw + 44, bh + 20, 2);
      const gy = by + bh + 9, gw = s.compact ? 180 : 220, gh = 12;
      const full = s.gauge >= 100;
      c.fillStyle = "#000";
      c.fillRect(bx - 2, gy - 2, gw + 4, gh + 4);
      bar(c, bx, gy, gw, gh, s.gauge / 100, full ? `hsl(${40 + Math.sin(this.t * 8) * 10},95%,${60 + Math.sin(this.t * 8) * 12}%)` : C.gaugeFill, "#10181c");
      if (full) {
        c.save();
        c.shadowColor = C.gauge;
        c.shadowBlur = 12 + Math.sin(this.t * 8) * 6;
        c.strokeStyle = C.gauge;
        c.lineWidth = 2;
        c.strokeRect(bx - 2, gy - 2, gw + 4, gh + 4);
        c.restore();
        label(c, s.device === "pad" ? "RT" : s.device === "touch" ? "필살" : superKey(), bx + gw + 10, gy + 12, 15, C.gauge, "left", 900);
      }
      if (s.weapon) this.ammo(c, s.weapon, bx, gy + gh + 30);
      this.meterRows(c, s, x, y + fs + 52);
    }
    meterRows(c, s, x, my) {
      for (const m of s.meters ?? []) {
        label(c, m.label, x, my + 10, 13, m.color, "left", 800);
        bar(c, x + 40, my + 1, 160, 9, m.v / m.max, m.color, "#000");
        my += 18;
      }
      let sx = x;
      for (const st of s.status ?? []) {
        c.font = font(13, 800);
        const w = c.measureText(st.label).width + 14;
        c.beginPath();
        c.roundRect(sx, my + 4, w, 22, 11);
        c.fillStyle = st.good ? "rgba(60,140,90,0.9)" : "rgba(150,50,50,0.9)";
        c.fill();
        label(c, st.label, sx + 7, my + 20, 13, "#fff", "left", 800);
        sx += w + 6;
      }
    }
    ammo(c, w, x, y) {
      const empty = w.mag <= 0;
      bullet(c, x + 6, y - 9, empty ? "#7a3a30" : "#f0d890");
      label(c, String(w.mag), x + 20, y, 26, empty ? C.hpLow : "#fff", "left", 900);
      c.font = font(26, 900);
      const mw = c.measureText(String(w.mag)).width;
      label(c, `예비 ${w.reserve}`, x + 26 + mw, y, 16, C.dim, "left", 800);
      c.font = font(16, 800);
      const ex = x + 34 + mw + c.measureText(`예비 ${w.reserve}`).width + 8;
      if (w.temp) label(c, `${w.temp.name} ${w.temp.uses}회`, ex, y, 15, C.amber, "left", 800);
      else if (w.reload != null) {
        bar(c, ex, y - 12, 90, 8, w.reload, "#e8e0b0", "#000");
        label(c, "재장전", ex + 98, y, 14, "#e8e0b0", "left", 800);
      } else if (w.mag <= Math.ceil(w.magMax * 0.25)) label(c, w.reserve > 0 ? "재장전!" : "탄 없음", ex, y, 15, Math.sin(this.t * 8) > 0 ? C.hpLow : "#fff", "left", 900);
    }
    /** 층: 오른쪽 위 구석에 글자만 (판 없음). 바뀔 때는 Fx 층 제목이 크게 알린다 */
    floor(c, s) {
      const f = s.floor;
      if (s.perks?.length) drawPerkChips(c, s.perks, W2 - (s.compact ? 100 : 20) - 170);
      if (s.field) {
        const r = W2 - (s.compact ? 100 : 20);
        if (s.field.clock) label(c, s.field.clock, r, 34, 26, "#fff", "right", 900);
        if (s.field.place) label(c, s.field.place, r, 56, 15, C.amber, "right", 800);
        if (s.field.wallet) label(c, s.field.wallet, r, 76, 15, "#cfe3c8", "right", 800);
      }
      if (!f.depth) return;
      const right = W2 - (s.compact ? 100 : 20);
      label(c, f.depth, right, 44, 32, "#fff", "right", 900);
      if (f.variant) label(c, f.variant, right, 68, 15, C.amber, "right", 800);
    }
    /** 아이템: 아이콘만 (글자 없음), 체력 막대 오른쪽 위 천장 띠. 바닥(물건·자막)과 겹치지 않게 (독립 검수: UI 안전영역).
     * 터치 화면에서는 터치 버튼이 대신 보여 준다 */
    /** 아이템 칸 넷 (도트, 필살 게이지 아래). 돌려주는 값 = 아래 끝 y */
    pixelItems(c, items, device, x, y) {
      return drawPixelItems(c, items, x, y, [], this.itemFlash, (it, ix, iy, sz) => drawHudItemIcon(c, it, ix, iy, sz));
    }
    items(c, items, device) {
      const n = 4, s = 52, g = 8;
      const x0 = 482, y = 22;
      const padKeys = ["↑", "→", "↓", "←"];
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const it = items[i] ?? { label: "", count: 0 };
        const x = x0 + i * (s + g);
        const dim2 = it.count <= 0;
        const sf = kitPiece("slot");
        if (sf) nine(c, sf, kitSlice("slot"), x - 4, y - 4, s + 8, s + 8, 1);
        else {
          c.fillStyle = "#000";
          c.fillRect(x - 2, y - 2, s + 4, s + 4);
          c.fillStyle = dim2 ? "rgba(30,34,38,0.8)" : "rgba(44,50,56,0.92)";
          c.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
        }
        if (this.itemFlash[i] > 0) c.fillStyle = `rgba(255,240,180,${this.itemFlash[i]})`, c.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
        if (it.label || it.id) {
          c.save();
          c.globalAlpha = dim2 ? 0.3 : 1;
          drawHudItemIcon(c, it, x + 2, y + 2, s - 4);
          c.restore();
        }
        label(c, device === "pad" ? padKeys[i] : String(i + 1), x + 4, y + 14, 12, C.dim, "left", 800);
        if (it.label || it.id) label(c, `${it.count}`, x + s - 4, y + s - 4, 17, dim2 ? "#6a7276" : "#fff", "right", 900);
        if (it.cd && it.cd > 0) {
          c.save();
          c.beginPath();
          c.moveTo(x + s / 2, y + s / 2);
          c.arc(x + s / 2, y + s / 2, s, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * it.cd);
          c.closePath();
          c.clip();
          c.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)";
          c.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
          c.restore();
        }
      }
    }
    boss(c, b, compact) {
      const w = 640, x = W2 / 2 - w / 2, y = H2 - 128;
      label(c, b.name, x, y - 6, 16, "#f0c0b0");
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.7)";
      c.fillRect(x, y, w, 14);
      c.fillStyle = "#b02a2a";
      c.fillRect(x, y, w * Math.max(0, b.hp) / b.max, 14);
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
      c.fillRect(x, y, w * Math.max(0, b.hp) / b.max, 4);
      c.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,200,180,0.5)";
      c.strokeRect(x + 0.5, y + 0.5, w - 1, 13);
    }
    timer(c, s) {
      if (!s.timer) return;
      const t = Math.max(0, s.timer.sec);
      const x = s.compact ? 340 : 482, y = s.compact ? 174 : 116;
      c.font = font(15);
      const text = fitText(c, `${s.timer.label} ${Math.floor(t / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(t % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`, s.compact ? 120 : 230);
      label(c, text, x, y, 15, t < 10 ? C.hpLow : "#bfe0f0");
    }
    /** 필드 주인공 칸 (전투 HUD가 없는 본부·거점): 왼쪽 아래 도트 얼굴 + 이름 + 체력 + 필살 게이지. 터치·좁은 화면은 왼쪽 위(아래는 스틱 자리).
     * 돌려주는 값 = 칸 오른쪽 끝 x (자막을 그 오른쪽에 둔다) */
    /** 필드(방문) 오른쪽 위: 필드 시각·장소·잔고 (전투 HUD를 안 그리는 필드에서) */
    drawFieldCorner(c, f, compact) {
      if (!f) return;
      c.save();
      const k = compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      const r = W2 - (compact ? 100 : 20);
      if (f.clock) label(c, f.clock, r, 34, 26, "#fff", "right", 900);
      if (f.place) label(c, f.place, r, 56, 15, C.amber, "right", 800);
      if (f.wallet) label(c, f.wallet, r, f.clock || f.place ? 76 : 34, 15, "#cfe3c8", "right", 800);
      c.restore();
    }
    drawFieldCard(c, s, dt = 1 / 60) {
      this.t += dt;
      c.save();
      const k = s.compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      H2 = c.canvas.height / k;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      pixelOn = true;
      const fs = 64, bw = 200, bh = 14;
      const x = 28, y = s.top ? 28 : H2 - fs - 28;
      if (this.lastHp >= 0 && s.hp < this.lastHp) this.hurtT = 0.35, this.trailHold = 0.5;
      if (this.trail < 0 || this.trail < s.hp) this.trail = s.hp;
      else if (this.trailHold > 0) this.trailHold -= dt;
      else this.trail = Math.max(s.hp, this.trail - s.maxHp * dt * 0.6);
      this.lastHp = s.hp;
      this.hurtT = Math.max(0, this.hurtT - dt);
      if (pixelHudReady()) {
        const cy = s.top ? 14 : H2 - 170;
        const end = drawPixelCard(c, { name: s.name, face: s.face, hp: s.hp, maxHp: s.maxHp, trail: this.trail, hurtT: this.hurtT, gauge: s.gauge, superKey: superKey(), t: this.t }, 16, cy);
        const iy = s.items && !s.compact ? this.pixelItems(c, s.items, s.device, 16 + 84, end + 6) : end;
        if (s.status?.length) statusChips(c, s.status, 16 + 84, iy + 8);
        pixelOn = false;
        c.restore();
        return (16 + 340) * k;
      }
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(6,10,14,0.72)";
      c.fillRect(x - 12, y - 12, fs + bw + 52, fs + 24);
      c.fillStyle = "#141b20";
      c.fillRect(x, y, fs, fs);
      c.save();
      c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      if (s.face) c.drawImage(s.face, x, y, fs, fs);
      else label(c, s.name.slice(0, 1) || "?", x + fs / 2, y + fs * 0.7, 34, "#fff", "center", 900);
      c.restore();
      if (this.hurtT > 0) c.fillStyle = `rgba(255,40,40,${this.hurtT * 1.2})`, c.fillRect(x, y, fs, fs);
      const pf = kitPiece("portrait");
      if (pf) nine(c, pf, kitSlice("portrait"), x - 8, y - 8, fs + 16, fs + 16, 1);
      else c.strokeStyle = C.frame, c.lineWidth = 3, c.strokeRect(x - 1.5, y - 1.5, fs + 3, fs + 3);
      const bx = x + fs + 18;
      label(c, s.name, bx, y + 16, 18, C.name, "left", 900);
      if (s.sub) {
        c.font = font(18, 900);
        label(c, s.sub, bx + c.measureText(s.name).width + 10, y + 16, 13, C.amber, "left", 800);
      }
      const by = y + 26;
      const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, s.hp / s.maxHp));
      c.fillStyle = "#000";
      c.fillRect(bx - 2, by - 2, bw + 4, bh + 4);
      c.fillStyle = "#3a1410";
      c.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
      c.fillStyle = C.trail;
      c.fillRect(bx, by, bw * this.trail / s.maxHp, bh);
      c.fillStyle = r < 0.25 ? Math.sin(this.t * 10) > 0 ? C.hpLow : C.hp : r < 0.5 ? C.hpMid : C.hp;
      c.fillRect(bx, by, Math.round(bw * r), bh);
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.35)";
      c.fillRect(bx, by + 2, Math.round(bw * r), 2);
      const bf = kitPiece("bar");
      if (bf) nine(c, bf, kitSlice("bar"), bx - 14, by - 7, bw + 28, bh + 14, 1);
      label(c, `${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s.hp))}`, bx + bw - 4, by + bh - 2, 12, "#fff", "right", 900);
      if (s.gauge !== void 0) {
        const gy = by + bh + 8, gw = 150, gh = 8, full = s.gauge >= 100;
        c.fillStyle = "#000";
        c.fillRect(bx - 2, gy - 2, gw + 4, gh + 4);
        bar(c, bx, gy, gw, gh, s.gauge / 100, full ? `hsl(${40 + Math.sin(this.t * 8) * 10},95%,${60 + Math.sin(this.t * 8) * 12}%)` : C.gaugeFill, "#10181c");
        if (full) label(c, `필살 ${superKey()}`, bx + gw + 8, gy + 9, 12, C.gauge, "left", 900);
      }
      pixelOn = false;
      c.restore();
      return (x + fs + bw + 40) * k;
    }
    /** 말풍선 (본부 대사): x = 말하는 사람 가운데, y = 머리 위(풍선 꼬리 끝), 캔버스 px. who가 있으면 위에 이름 */
    drawSpeech(c, p) {
      c.save();
      const k = p.compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      pixelOn = true;
      const U2 = 2;
      c.font = font(22);
      const lines = wrapText(c, p.text, 400, 3);
      const tw = Math.max(...lines.map((l) => Math.ceil(c.measureText(l).width)), p.who ? Math.ceil(c.measureText(p.who).width) * 0.55 : 0);
      const nameH = p.who ? 18 : 0, lh = 26, padX = 12, padY = 8;
      const w = tw + padX * 2, h = nameH + lines.length * lh + padY * 2 - 4;
      const tail = 10;
      let x = Math.round(p.x / k - w / 2);
      x = Math.max(8, Math.min(W2 - w - 8, x));
      const y = Math.round(Math.max(8, p.y / k - tail - h));
      const box2 = (bx, by, bw, bh, col) => {
        c.fillStyle = col;
        c.fillRect(bx + U2, by, bw - 2 * U2, bh);
        c.fillRect(bx, by + U2, bw, bh - 2 * U2);
      };
      box2(x + U2, y + U2 * 2, w, h, "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)");
      box2(x - U2, y - U2, w + 2 * U2, h + 2 * U2, "#0b0f12");
      box2(x, y, w, h, "#cfc8b4");
      box2(x + U2, y + U2, w - 2 * U2, h - 2 * U2, "#1a2026");
      const tx = Math.round(Math.max(x + 10, Math.min(x + w - 18, p.x / k - 4)));
      for (let i = 0; i < tail / U2; i++) {
        const tw2 = Math.max(U2, 10 - i * U2 * 2);
        c.fillStyle = "#0b0f12";
        c.fillRect(tx + (10 - tw2) / 2 - U2, y + h + i * U2 - U2, tw2 + 2 * U2, U2 + U2);
        c.fillStyle = "#cfc8b4";
        c.fillRect(tx + (10 - tw2) / 2, y + h + i * U2 - U2, tw2, U2);
      }
      c.textAlign = "left";
      c.textBaseline = "middle";
      let ty = y + padY + 2;
      if (p.who) {
        c.font = font(11);
        c.fillStyle = "#ffcf6a";
        c.fillText(p.who, x + padX, ty + 6);
        ty += nameH;
      }
      c.font = font(22);
      c.fillStyle = "#f2ecdc";
      lines.forEach((l, i) => c.fillText(l, x + padX, ty + lh / 2 + i * lh - 2));
      pixelOn = false;
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 상호작용 안내: 캐릭터 옆에 뜨는 도트 말풍선 (키 단추 + 짧은 말). x,y = 캐릭터 머리 옆 화면 px(캔버스 px), side = 말풍선이 뻗는 쪽 */
    drawPrompt(c, p) {
      c.save();
      const k = p.compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      pixelOn = true;
      const U2 = 2;
      c.font = font(22);
      const tw = Math.ceil(c.measureText(p.text).width);
      const cap = 30, gap = 8, padX = 10, h = 40;
      const w = padX + cap + gap + tw + padX + (p.locked ? 20 : 0);
      const bob = Math.round(Math.sin(performance.now() / 260) * 2);
      let x = Math.round(p.x / k + (p.side > 0 ? 0 : -w)), y = Math.round(p.y / k - h / 2 + bob);
      const box2 = (bx, by, bw, bh, col) => {
        c.fillStyle = col;
        c.fillRect(bx + U2, by, bw - 2 * U2, bh);
        c.fillRect(bx, by + U2, bw, bh - 2 * U2);
      };
      box2(x + U2, y + U2 * 2, w, h, "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)");
      box2(x - U2, y - U2, w + 2 * U2, h + 2 * U2, "#0b0f12");
      box2(x, y, w, h, p.locked ? "#c98a1c" : "#cfc8b4");
      box2(x + U2, y + U2, w - 2 * U2, h - 2 * U2, "#1a2026");
      const tx = p.side > 0 ? x - U2 * 3 : x + w + U2;
      c.fillStyle = p.locked ? "#c98a1c" : "#cfc8b4";
      c.fillRect(tx, y + h / 2 - U2, U2 * 2, U2 * 2);
      c.fillRect(p.side > 0 ? tx + U2 * 2 : tx - U2, y + h / 2 - U2 * 2, U2, U2 * 4);
      const kx = x + padX, ky = y + (h - cap) / 2;
      box2(kx, ky + U2, cap, cap, "#6e6656");
      box2(kx, ky, cap, cap - U2, p.locked ? "#5a4a2a" : "#f2ead6");
      c.font = font(22);
      c.fillStyle = p.locked ? "#c9b48a" : "#1a2026";
      c.textAlign = "center";
      c.textBaseline = "middle";
      c.fillText(p.key, kx + cap / 2, ky + cap / 2);
      let txx = kx + cap + gap;
      if (p.locked) {
        c.fillStyle = "#ffcf6a";
        c.fillRect(txx + 2, y + 18, 12, 10);
        c.fillRect(txx + 4, y + 12, 2, 6);
        c.fillRect(txx + 10, y + 12, 2, 6);
        c.fillRect(txx + 4, y + 11, 8, 2);
        c.fillStyle = "#1a2026";
        c.fillRect(txx + 7, y + 21, 2, 4);
        txx += 20;
      }
      c.textAlign = "left";
      c.fillStyle = p.locked ? "#ffcf6a" : "#f2ecdc";
      c.fillText(p.text, txx, y + h / 2 + 1);
      pixelOn = false;
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 왼쪽 아래 기술 칸 줄 (도트, PixelLab 틀). 돌려주는 값 = 오른쪽 끝 x (캔버스 px) */
    drawSkillBox(c, s, dt = 1 / 60) {
      this.t += dt;
      if (!pixelHudReady()) return 0;
      c.save();
      const k = s.compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      H2 = c.canvas.height / k;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      const sz = hotbarSize(s.slots.length);
      const end = drawPixelHotbar(c, s.slots, 16, H2 - sz.h - 10, this.t);
      if (s.caption) {
        c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
        c.drawImage(pixelText(s.caption, "#ffe9a0", 11), 16, Math.round(H2 - sz.h - 10 - 16));
      }
      c.restore();
      return end * k;
    }
    /** 아래 자막 한 줄만 (본부처럼 전투 HUD가 없는 화면) */
    drawMessage(c, text, compact, left = 0) {
      c.save();
      const k = compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      H2 = c.canvas.height / k;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      this.message(c, text, compact, left / k);
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 본부의 전투 HUD 없는 화면에서도 동일한 안내 영역을 쓴다. */
    drawNavigation(c, navigation, compact = false, device) {
      c.save();
      const k = compact ? 1.2 : 1;
      W2 = c.canvas.width / k;
      H2 = c.canvas.height / k;
      c.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, 0, 0);
      pixelOn = true;
      this.navigation(c, { navigation, compact, device }, 20);
      pixelOn = false;
      c.restore();
    }
    message(c, text, compact, left = 0) {
      c.font = font(17, 600);
      let t = text;
      const cx = left ? (left + W2) / 2 : W2 / 2;
      t = fitText(c, t, Math.min(900, left ? W2 - left - 40 : W2 - (compact ? 240 : 64)));
      const w = c.measureText(t).width + 40;
      const y = H2 - 44;
      plate2(c, cx - w / 2, y, w, 36, 18);
      label(c, t, cx, y + 25, 17, "#fff8e6", "center", 600);
    }
    navigation(c, s, top) {
      const nav = s.navigation;
      if (!nav && !s.prompt) return;
      const y = top ?? (s.compact ? 220 : 204);
      const x = s.compact ? 100 : 20, width = W2 - x * 2;
      const lines = [];
      if (nav?.objective) lines.push({ text: `현재 목표 · ${nav.objective}`, color: C.rule });
      const target = nav?.target;
      if (target) {
        const key = target.kind === "locked" ? "잠김" : target.kind === "decoration" ? "장식" : s.device === "pad" ? "B" : s.device === "touch" || s.compact ? "조사" : "E";
        lines.push({ text: `[${key}] ${target.text}${target.reason ? ` · ${target.reason}` : ""}`, color: target.kind === "locked" ? C.amber : C.text });
      } else if (s.prompt) {
        const key = s.device === "pad" ? "B" : s.device === "touch" || s.compact ? "조사" : s.prompt.key;
        lines.push({ text: `[${key}] ${s.prompt.text}`, color: C.text });
      }
      if (!lines.length) return;
      c.font = font(15);
      const rows = lines.flatMap((line2) => wrapText(c, line2.text.replace(/[①②③④]/g, (ch) => `${"①②③④".indexOf(ch) + 1}번`), width - 64, 2).map((text) => ({ ...line2, text })));
      plate2(c, x, y, width, rows.length * 28 + 14);
      rows.forEach((row, i) => label(c, row.text, x + 12, y + 27 + i * 28, 15, row.color));
      if (target) {
        c.save();
        c.translate(x + width - 24, y + rows.length * 28 - 2);
        c.scale(0.4, 0.4);
        drawHintIcon(c, 0, 0, 0, target.kind === "locked" ? "locked" : target.kind === "decoration" ? "decoration" : "available");
        c.restore();
      }
    }
    drawToasts(c, dt) {
      let y = this.last?.navigation || this.last?.prompt ? (this.last.compact ? 220 : 204) + 154 : 196;
      for (const t of this.toasts) {
        t.t += dt;
        const a = t.t < 0.15 ? t.t / 0.15 : t.t > 2.2 ? Math.max(0, 1 - (t.t - 2.2) / 0.5) : 1;
        c.globalAlpha = a;
        const x = this.last?.compact ? 100 : 22;
        c.font = font(15);
        label(c, fitText(c, t.text, W2 - x * 2), x + (1 - Math.min(1, t.t / 0.15)) * -20, y, 15, t.color);
        c.globalAlpha = 1;
        y += 22;
      }
      this.toasts = this.toasts.filter((t) => t.t < 2.7);
    }
  };
  var shared = new Hud();
  function bullet(c, x, y, color) {
    c.fillStyle = "#000";
    c.fillRect(x - 5, y - 12, 10, 26);
    c.fillStyle = color;
    c.beginPath();
    c.moveTo(x - 3, y + 12);
    c.lineTo(x - 3, y - 4);
    c.quadraticCurveTo(x, y - 12, x + 3, y - 4);
    c.lineTo(x + 3, y + 12);
    c.closePath();
    c.fill();
    c.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)";
    c.fillRect(x - 3, y + 4, 6, 2);
  }

  function drawHudItemIcon(c, it, x, y, size2) {
    const src = it.id ? ITEM_IMG[it.id] ?? (/탄약|탄창/.test(it.label) ? ITEM_IMG.ammo : ITEM_IMG.unknown) : null;
    const im = src ? kitImage(src) : null;
    if (im) {
      const k = Math.max(1, Math.floor(size2 / 32));
      const d3 = 32 * k;
      c.save();
      c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      c.drawImage(im, Math.round(x + (size2 - d3) / 2), Math.round(y + (size2 - d3) / 2), d3, d3);
      c.restore();
      return;
    }
    if (it.id && iconDrawer) return iconDrawer(c, it.id, x, y, size2);
    c.save();
    c.translate(x, y);
    c.scale(size2 / 32, size2 / 32);
    const l = it.label;
    const f = (col, ...r) => {
      c.fillStyle = col;
      for (let i = 0; i < r.length; i += 4) c.fillRect(r[i], r[i + 1], r[i + 2], r[i + 3]);
    };
    if (/붕대|봉대|밴드/.test(l)) f("#e8e2d0", 4, 11, 24, 10), f("#c9c0a8", 4, 11, 24, 2, 4, 19, 24, 2), f("#d65a5a", 14, 13, 4, 6, 13, 14, 6, 4);
    else if (/섬광|수류|폭탄|탄$/.test(l)) {
      f("#e08a3c", 9, 10, 14, 18), f("#9aa4ab", 12, 5, 8, 6), f("#4a5258", 19, 3, 6, 3), f("#fff3b0", 12, 14, 3, 8);
    } else if (/주사|각성|제$/.test(l)) {
      f("#9aa4ab", 6, 24, 4, 4), f("#e8ecee", 9, 9, 8, 16), f("#9a7fd1", 10, 14, 6, 10), f("#4a5258", 11, 5, 4, 5, 8, 3, 10, 3), f("#d8d8d8", 12, 25, 2, 6);
    } else if (/약|물약|회복|포션/.test(l)) {
      f("#4a5258", 13, 3, 6, 4), f("#c8d0d4", 12, 7, 8, 4), f("#5fbf6a", 7, 11, 18, 18), f("#9be0a3", 9, 13, 4, 8), f("#e8ecee", 13, 17, 6, 2, 15, 15, 2, 6);
    } else if (/부적|봉인|종이/.test(l)) f("#e6d36a", 8, 4, 16, 24), f("#d65a5a", 11, 8, 10, 2, 15, 8, 2, 16, 11, 16, 10, 2);
    else if (/열쇠|키$/.test(l)) f("#d6b64a", 4, 10, 10, 10, 14, 14, 16, 3, 24, 17, 3, 5, 19, 17, 3, 4), f("#2c383e", 7, 13, 4, 4);
    else if (/손전등|등$/.test(l)) f("#4a5258", 4, 12, 16, 8), f("#9aa4ab", 20, 9, 6, 14), f("#fff3b0", 26, 10, 3, 12);
    else {
      f("#a07850", 6, 9, 20, 18), f("#7a5838", 6, 9, 20, 4), f("#d6b64a", 14, 9, 4, 18);
    }
    c.restore();
  }
  function statusHtml(s) {
    if (!s) return "";
    const esc6 = (t) => t.replace(/[&<>"]/g, (ch) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[ch]);
    const rows = [
      s.floor.depth || s.floor.name ? `<tr><th>층</th><td>${esc6((s.floor.depth && !s.floor.name.startsWith(s.floor.depth) ? [s.floor.depth, s.floor.name] : [s.floor.name || s.floor.depth]).filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}${s.floor.variant ? ` · ${esc6(s.floor.variant)}` : ""}</td></tr>` : ""
    ];
    return `<table>${rows.join("")}</table>`;
  }
  function superKey() {
    const code = Object.keys(KEYMAP).find((k) => KEYMAP[k] === "super");
    return code ? code.replace(/^Key/, "") : "W";
  }

  var K = 3;
  var DOOR2 = { x: 160 + 112 * K, y: 50 * K, w: 100 * K, h: 170 * K };
  var LAMP = { x: 160 + 132 * K, y: 17 * K, w: 48 * K, h: 10 * K };
  var imgs2 = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function art(name) {
    if (!imgs2.has(name)) {
      const src = typeof window !== "undefined" && window.__GOEI_BG_ART__?.el_hall?.[name] || "";
      let im2 = null;
      if (src) im2 = new Image(), im2.src = src;
      imgs2.set(name, im2);
    }
    const im = imgs2.get(name);
    return im && im.complete && im.naturalWidth ? im : null;
  }
  var textCache2 = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function pixelText2(text, size2, top, bottom, weight = 900, pixel = false) {
    const ready = pixel && typeof document !== "undefined" && document.fonts?.check(`11px "${PIXEL_FONT}"`);
    const key = `${text}|${size2}|${top}|${bottom}|${weight}|${pixel}|${ready}`;
    const hit2 = textCache2.get(key);
    if (hit2) return hit2;
    const m = document.createElement("canvas").getContext("2d");
    m.font = pixel ? `${size2}px "${PIXEL_FONT}", ${FONT}` : `${weight} ${size2}px ${FONT}`;
    const w = Math.ceil(m.measureText(text).width) + 6, h = Math.ceil(size2 * 1.3) + 6;
    const mask = document.createElement("canvas");
    mask.width = w;
    mask.height = h;
    const g = mask.getContext("2d");
    g.font = m.font;
    g.textBaseline = pixel ? "top" : "middle";
    g.fillStyle = "#fff";
    g.fillText(text, 3, pixel ? 3 : h / 2 + 1);
    const d3 = g.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
    for (let i = 3; i < d3.data.length; i += 4) d3.data[i] = d3.data[i] > 110 ? 255 : 0;
    g.putImageData(d3, 0, 0);
    g.globalCompositeOperation = "source-in";
    const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, h * 0.25, 0, h * 0.8);
    gr.addColorStop(0, top);
    gr.addColorStop(0.55, top);
    gr.addColorStop(0.56, bottom);
    gr.addColorStop(1, bottom);
    g.fillStyle = gr;
    g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const out = document.createElement("canvas");
    out.width = w + 2;
    out.height = h + 4;
    const o = out.getContext("2d");
    const black = document.createElement("canvas");
    black.width = w;
    black.height = h;
    const b = black.getContext("2d");
    b.drawImage(mask, 0, 0);
    b.globalCompositeOperation = "source-in";
    b.fillStyle = "#000";
    b.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    for (const [dx, dy] of [[0, 3], [1, 3], [2, 3], [0, 2], [2, 2]]) o.drawImage(black, dx, dy);
    for (const [dx, dy] of [[0, 1], [2, 1], [1, 0], [1, 2], [0, 0], [2, 0], [0, 2], [2, 2]]) o.drawImage(black, dx, dy);
    o.drawImage(mask, 1, 1);
    textCache2.set(key, out);
    return out;
  }
  function blit(c, im, cx, y, k, alpha = 1) {
    c.globalAlpha = alpha;
    c.drawImage(im, Math.round(cx - im.width * k / 2), Math.round(y), im.width * k, im.height * k);
    c.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
  function drawTitleBg(c, t, title2 = "괴이 ː ANOMALY", sub = "필드") {
    c.save();
    c.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    const W5 = c.canvas.width, H5 = c.canvas.height;
    const sx = W5 / 1280, sy = H5 / 720;
    c.scale(sx, sy);
    const wall = art("el_hall_wall_1");
    if (wall) {
      c.fillStyle = "#0a0806";
      c.fillRect(0, 0, 1280, 720);
      c.drawImage(wall, 160, 0, 320 * K, 220 * K);
      const l = art("el_hall_wall_4"), r = art("el_hall_wall_2");
      const cut = 160 / K;
      if (l) c.drawImage(l, 320 - cut, 0, cut, 220, 0, 0, 160, 220 * K);
      if (r) c.drawImage(r, 0, 0, cut, 220, 1120, 0, 160, 220 * K);
      const fl = art("el_hall_floor");
      if (fl) {
        const p = c.createPattern(fl, "repeat");
        if (p) {
          p.setTransform(new DOMMatrix([K, 0, 0, K / 2, 0, 660]));
          c.fillStyle = p;
          c.fillRect(0, 660, 1280, 60);
        }
      }
    } else drawFallback(c, t);
    const buzz = Math.sin(t * 23) > 0.96 || Math.sin(t * 1.3) > 0.985 && Math.sin(t * 40) > 0;
    c.fillStyle = `rgba(4,6,10,${buzz ? 0.72 : 0.5})`;
    c.fillRect(0, 0, 1280, 720);
    const d3 = DOOR2;
    c.save();
    c.beginPath();
    c.rect(d3.x, d3.y, d3.w, d3.h);
    c.clip();
    c.fillStyle = "#030203";
    c.fillRect(d3.x, d3.y, d3.w, d3.h);
    const breath = 0.35 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 1.4) + (Math.sin(t * 9) > 0.97 ? 0.3 : 0);
    const rg = c.createRadialGradient(d3.x + d3.w / 2, d3.y + d3.h + 40, 10, d3.x + d3.w / 2, d3.y + d3.h + 40, d3.h * 0.95);
    rg.addColorStop(0, `rgba(200,36,20,${breath})`);
    rg.addColorStop(1, "rgba(60,8,6,0)");
    c.fillStyle = rg;
    c.fillRect(d3.x, d3.y, d3.w, d3.h);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const fy = d3.y + d3.h - 60 - i * 70 + Math.sin(t * 0.7 + i) * 12;
      c.fillStyle = `rgba(120,30,24,${0.08 + 0.04 * Math.sin(t + i * 2)})`;
      c.fillRect(d3.x, Math.round(fy / K) * K, d3.w, K * 4);
    }
    c.restore();
    const lp = LAMP;
    c.fillStyle = "#0c0302";
    c.fillRect(lp.x, lp.y, lp.w, lp.h);
    const flick = Math.sin(t * 13) > 0.9 || Math.sin(t * 2.1) > 0.97;
    const floors = ["1", "B1", "B3", "B7", "B12", "??"];
    const num = pixelText2(flick ? floors[Math.floor(t * 9) % floors.length] : floors[Math.floor(t / 2.5) % floors.length], 9, flick ? "#ff3020" : "#ff7a3a", flick ? "#c01810" : "#e05a20");
    c.save();
    c.shadowColor = "#ff4020";
    c.shadowBlur = 14;
    blit(c, num, lp.x + lp.w / 2, lp.y - 3, 2);
    c.restore();
    const v = c.createRadialGradient(640, 380, 220, 640, 380, 820);
    v.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
    v.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0.8)");

  }
  function drawFallback(c, t) {
    const g = c.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 720);
    g.addColorStop(0, "#2a2620");
    g.addColorStop(1, "#14100c");
    c.fillStyle = g;
    c.fillRect(0, 0, 1280, 720);
    c.fillStyle = "#3a342a";
    c.fillRect(0, 660, 1280, 60);
    const d3 = DOOR2;
    c.fillStyle = "#6a5a3a";
    c.fillRect(d3.x - 18, d3.y - 18, d3.w + 36, d3.h + 18);
    c.fillStyle = "#000";
    c.fillRect(d3.x, d3.y, d3.w, d3.h);
    c.fillStyle = "#6a5a3a";
    c.fillRect(LAMP.x - 6, LAMP.y - 6, LAMP.w + 12, LAMP.h + 12);
    void t;
  }
  function drawGoSign(c, x, y, t) {
    c.save();
    c.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    c.translate(x, y);
    c.scale(0.62, 0.62);
    c.translate(-x, -y);
    const im = pixelText2("전진", 33, "#ffe9a8", "#ffb43a", 700, true);
    const k = 2;
    {
      const pw = im.width * k + 12 + 9 * 4 + 8 + 28, ph = Math.max(im.height * k, 19 * 4) + 20;
      const px = Math.round(x - 14) & ~3, py = Math.round(y + im.height * k / 2 - ph / 2) & ~3;
      c.fillStyle = "#0c0f12";
      c.fillRect(px - 4, py - 4, pw + 8, ph + 8);
      c.fillStyle = "#b8873a";
      c.fillRect(px, py, pw, ph);
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(16,20,24,0.92)";
      c.fillRect(px + 4, py + 4, pw - 8, ph - 8);
    }
    c.drawImage(im, Math.round(x), Math.round(y), im.width * k, im.height * k);
    const ax = Math.round(x + im.width * k + 12 + Math.floor(t * 6) % 2 * 8), ay = Math.round(y + im.height * k / 2);
    const P2 = 4;
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const h = 9 - i;
      c.fillStyle = "#000";
      c.fillRect(ax + i * P2 - P2, ay - h * P2 - P2, P2 * 2, (h * 2 + 1) * P2 + P2 * 2);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const h = 9 - i;
      c.fillStyle = i < 5 ? "#ffe9a8" : "#ffb43a";
      c.fillRect(ax + i * P2, ay - h * P2, P2, (h * 2 - 1) * P2 + P2);
    }
    c.restore();
  }

  // src/game/action/engine/bodies.ts
  var Dummy = class extends Fighter {
    constructor(x, y, endless = true) {
      super();
      this.endless = endless;
      if (!endless) this.hp = this.hpMax = 70;
      this.x = this.px = x;
      this.y = this.py = y;
      this.dir = -1;
    }
    team = "enemy";
    kind = "dummy";
    label = "허수아비";
    hp = 1e3;
    hpMax = 1e3;
    regen = 0;
    frame() {
      if (this.phase === "dead") return { hit: [], hurt: [] };
      const lying = this.phase === "down";
      return { hit: [], hurt: [toWorld(this, -22, 22, 0, lying ? 30 : 170)] };
    }
    applyHit(hit2, world) {
      const r = super.applyHit(hit2, world);
      if (this.endless) {
        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hp = 1;
        if (this.phase === "dead") this.enter("down", 20);
        this.regen = 90;
      }
      return { kind: r.kind };
    }
    step(world) {
      if (this.regen > 0 && --this.regen === 0) this.hp = this.hpMax;
      if (this.stepReact(world)) return;
      this.vx = 0;
    }
  };
  var Thug = class extends Fighter {
    team = "enemy";
    kind;
    ai = "wait";
    aiT = 0;
    atk = 0;
    hitDone = 0;
    strafe = 0;
    elite;
    speed;
    dmg;
    constructor(spec, world) {
      super();
      this.kind = spec.kind;
      this.elite = !!spec.elite;
      this.boss = !!spec.boss;
      this.label = spec.boss ? "낡은 제복의 경비원" : spec.elite ? "정예 · 해롭지 않아 보이는 것" : "해롭지 않아 보이는 것";
      this.hp = this.hpMax = spec.boss ? 420 : spec.elite ? 110 : 60;
      this.speed = spec.boss ? 2.6 : 3.2;
      this.dmg = spec.boss ? 18 : spec.elite ? 14 : 10;
      this.armor = spec.boss ? 1 : 0;
      this.grabbable = !spec.boss;
      this.x = this.px = spec.x;
      this.y = this.py = spec.y;
      this.aiT = Math.floor(world.rand() * 30);
      this.strafe = world.rand() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
    }
    get tall() {
      return this.boss ? 200 : 168;
    }
    frame() {
      if (this.phase === "dead") return { hit: [], hurt: [] };
      const lying = this.phase === "down" || this.phase === "air-hurt" && this.z < 20;
      const hurt2 = [toWorld(this, -24, 24, 0, lying ? 34 : this.tall)];
      const hit2 = [];
      if (this.phase === "free" && this.ai === "strike" && this.aiT < 3) {
        const reach = this.boss ? 120 : 92;
        hit2.push({ ...toWorld(this, 10, reach, 90, 140, 16), attackId: this.atk, dmg: this.dmg, react: this.boss ? "down" : "stagger", dir: this.dir, push: this.boss ? 10 : 6, lift: 10, stop: 4, strength: 2 });
      }
      return { hit: hit2, hurt: hurt2 };
    }
    onKilled(world) {
      world.emit({ type: "enemyDie", x: this.x, kind: this.kind });
    }
    step(world) {
      if (this.boss) this.armor = this.ai === "windup" || this.ai === "strike" ? 2 : 1;
      if (this.stepReact(world)) {
        if (this.phase !== "free") this.ai = "recover";
        this.aiT = 0;
        return;
      }
      const p = world.player;
      const dx = p.x - this.x;
      const dy = p.y - this.y;
      this.aiT++;
      switch (this.ai) {
        case "wait":
        case "recover": {
          this.vx = 0;
          if (this.aiT > (this.ai === "recover" ? 18 : 10)) this.set("approach");
          break;
        }
        case "approach": {
          if (p.hp <= 0) return;
          this.dir = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
          const want = this.boss ? 100 : 78;
          const okX = Math.abs(dx) < want + 8;
          const okY = Math.abs(dy) < 10;
          const tx = okX ? 0 : Math.sign(dx) * this.speed;
          const ty = okY ? 0 : Math.sign(dy) * this.speed * 0.6;
          this.x += tx;
          this.y += ty + this.strafe * 0.15;
          if (okX && okY && this.aiT > 6) {
            this.set("windup");
            world.emit({ type: this.boss ? "bossTell" : "enemyTell", x: this.x, kind: this.kind });
          }
          if (this.aiT > 200) this.set("wait");
          break;
        }
        case "windup":
          if (this.aiT >= (this.boss ? 18 : 13)) {
            this.atk = newAttackId();
            this.hitDone = 0;
            this.set("strike");
            this.x += this.dir * 6;
          }
          break;
        case "strike":
          if (this.aiT >= (this.boss ? 14 : 12)) this.set("recover");
          break;
      }
      const [lo, hi] = world.lane;
      this.y = Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, this.y));
      void this.hitDone;
    }
    set(a) {
      this.ai = a;
      this.aiT = 0;
    }
    draw(ctx) {
      const h = this.tall;
      const w = 48;
      const lying = this.phase === "down" || this.phase === "dead";
      const tell = this.ai === "windup" && this.phase === "free";
      ctx.save();
      if (this.phase === "dead") ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, 1 - this.t / 30) * (this.t % 4 < 2 ? 1 : 0.4);
      const body = this.flash > 0 ? "#ffffff" : tell && this.aiT % 4 < 2 ? "#c84040" : this.boss ? "#5d5a52" : this.elite ? "#6a6070" : "#77818a";
      ctx.fillStyle = body;
      if (lying) ctx.fillRect(-h / 2, -30, h, 30);
      else {
        ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h, w, h);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#20262a";
        ctx.fillRect(this.dir * 8 - 4, -h + 18, 8, 6);
        ctx.fillStyle = body;
        const arm = this.phase === "free" && this.ai === "strike" ? this.boss ? 110 : 84 : tell ? -14 : 18;
        ctx.fillRect(this.dir > 0 ? 6 : -6 - arm, -120, arm, 16);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
  };
  var Bullet = class {
    constructor(x, y, z, dir) {
      this.x = x;
      this.y = y;
      this.z = z;
      this.dir = dir;
      this.px = x;
      this.py = y;
      this.pz = z;
    }
    id = newId();
    team = "player";
    kind = "bullet";
    attackId = newAttackId();
    hp = 1;
    hpMax = 1;
    alive = true;
    stop = 0;
    dist = 0;
    px;
    py;
    pz;
    frame() {
      const x0 = this.dir > 0 ? this.x - 44 : this.x;
      return {
        hit: [{ x0, x1: x0 + 44, z0: this.z - 10, z1: this.z + 10, y: this.y, dy: 14, attackId: this.attackId, dmg: 7, react: "flinch", dir: this.dir, push: 2, stop: 2, strength: 1, noise: 0.6 }],
        hurt: []
      };
    }
    step(world) {
      this.x += this.dir * 44;
      this.dist += 44;
      const [b0, b1] = world.bounds;
      if (this.dist > 1100 || this.x < b0 - 100 || this.x > b1 + 100) this.alive = false;
    }
    applyHit() {
      return { kind: "immune" };
    }
    draw(ctx) {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ffe9a0";
      ctx.fillRect(this.dir > 0 ? -40 : 0, -2, 40, 4);
    }
  };

  // src/game/action/art/items.ts
  var ALIAS2 = { heal: "heal_orb", ammo: "rounds", coin: "coins" };
  var cache3 = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function art2(id) {
    const all = typeof window !== "undefined" && window.__GOEI_ITEM_ART__ || {};
    return all[id] ?? all[ALIAS2[id] ?? ""];
  }
  function img5(a) {
    let im = cache3.get(a.src);
    if (!im) {
      im = new Image();
      im.src = a.src;
      cache3.set(a.src, im);
    }
    return im.complete && im.naturalWidth ? im : null;
  }
  function drawItemArt(ctx, id, x, y, size2) {
    const a = art2(id);
    const im = a && img5(a);
    if (!a || !im) return false;
    const fit2 = Math.min(size2 / a.w, size2 / a.h);
    const k = fit2 >= 1 ? Math.floor(fit2) : fit2;
    const w = a.w * k, h = a.h * k;
    const prev = ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled;
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.drawImage(im, Math.round(x + (size2 - w) / 2), Math.round(y + size2 - h), w, h);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = prev;
    return true;
  }
  function drawItemHeld(ctx, id, deg) {
    const a = art2(id);
    const im = a && img5(a);
    if (!a || !im || !a.grip) return false;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.rotate((deg - 90) * Math.PI / 180);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.drawImage(im, -a.grip[0] * 2, -a.grip[1] * 2, a.w * 2, a.h * 2);
    ctx.restore();
    return true;
  }
