  // src/game/action/items/inventory.ts
  var SLOT_COUNT = 4;
  function createItemRun(agent, loadout) {
    const sig = SIGNATURES[agent] ?? SIGNATURES.jyaldi;
    const slots = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < SLOT_COUNT; i++) {
      const s = loadout?.[i];
      slots.push(s && ITEMS[s.id] && Number.isFinite(s.n) && s.n >= 1 ? { id: s.id, n: Math.min(ITEMS[s.id].max, Math.floor(s.n)) } : null);
    }
    return {
      agent: sig.agent,
      loadout: slots,
      mag: sig.mag,
      reserve: sig.reserve,
      reloadT: 0,
      shotCd: 0,
      held: null,
      carry: null,
      ward: 0,
      itemCd: 0
    };
  }
  function addItem(run, id, n = 1) {
    const def = ITEMS[id];
    if (!def || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n <= 0) return 0;
    let left = n;
    for (const s of run.loadout) {
      if (s && s.id === id && s.n < def.max) {
        const k = Math.min(left, def.max - s.n);
        s.n += k;
        left -= k;
        if (!left) return n;
      }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < run.loadout.length && left; i++) {
      if (!run.loadout[i]) {
        const k = Math.min(left, def.max);
        run.loadout[i] = { id, n: k };
        left -= k;
      }
    }
    return n - left;
  }
  function useless(effect, c) {
    if (!c) return false;
    switch (effect.type) {
      case "heal":
        return c.hp >= c.max || !!c.noHeal;
      case "cure":
        if (effect.status === "bleed") return !c.bleed && c.hp >= c.max;
        if (effect.status === "taint") return !c.taint;
        if (effect.status === "headache") return !c.headache;
        if (effect.status === "mind") return !c.mind;
        return false;
      case "energy":
        return c.energy !== void 0 && c.energyMax !== void 0 && c.energy >= c.energyMax;
      case "gauge":
        return c.gauge !== void 0 && c.gauge >= 1;
      default:
        return false;
    }
  }
  function useItem(run, slot, ctx) {
    const s = run.loadout[slot];
    if (!s || s.n <= 0 || run.itemCd > 0) return null;
    const def = ITEMS[s.id];
    if (!def || !def.always && useless(def.effect, ctx)) return null;
    s.n--;
    if (s.n <= 0) run.loadout[slot] = null;
    run.itemCd = 0.35;
    if (def.effect.type === "ward") run.ward += def.effect.hits;
    return { id: def.id, effect: def.effect };
  }
  var pickupHealAmount = (max, amount = 1) => max * 0.08 * amount;
  var usesAmmo = (run) => (SIGNATURES[run.agent]?.ammo ?? "none") !== "none";
  function addAmmo(run, amount) {
    const sig = SIGNATURES[run.agent];
    if (!sig || sig.ammo === "none") return 0;
    const want = amount ?? AMMO_PICKUP[sig.ammo].amount;
    const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(want, sig.reserveMax - run.reserve));
    run.reserve += k;
    return k;
  }
  function fireSignature(run) {
    if (!usesAmmo(run)) return "free";
    if (run.reloadT > 0) return "reloading";
    if (run.shotCd > 0) return "cooldown";
    if (run.mag <= 0) {
      if (startReload(run)) return "reloading";
      return "dry";
    }
    run.mag--;
    run.shotCd = 0.16;
    if (run.mag === 0) startReload(run);
    return "fire";
  }
  function startReload(run) {
    const sig = SIGNATURES[run.agent];
    if (!sig || sig.ammo === "none" || run.reloadT > 0 || run.reserve <= 0 || run.mag >= sig.mag) return false;
    run.reloadT = sig.reload;
    return true;
  }
  function tickRun(run, dt) {
    run.itemCd = Math.max(0, run.itemCd - dt);
    run.shotCd = Math.max(0, run.shotCd - dt);
    if (run.reloadT > 0) {
      run.reloadT -= dt;
      if (run.reloadT <= 0) {
        run.reloadT = 0;
        const sig = SIGNATURES[run.agent];
        const k = Math.min(sig.mag - run.mag, run.reserve);
        run.mag += k;
        run.reserve -= k;
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  function equip(run, id, uses) {
    const def = WEAPONS[id];
    if (!def) return null;
    const prev = run.held;
    run.held = { id, uses: uses ?? def.uses };
    return prev;
  }
  function useWeapon(run, cls) {
    const h = run.held;
    if (!h) return null;
    const def = WEAPONS[h.id];
    if (def.cls !== cls) return null;
    h.uses--;
    const broke = h.uses <= 0;
    if (broke) run.held = null;
    return { def, broke, left: Math.max(0, h.uses) };
  }

  // src/game/action/items/field.ts
  function rand(f) {
    let t = f.seed = f.seed + 1831565813 | 0;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  }
  var pick = (f, a) => a[Math.floor(rand(f) * a.length)];
  function createField(seed = 1, depth = 1, bounds = { x0: 40, x1: 1240, y0: 480, y1: 680 }) {
    return { seed: seed | 0 || 1, nextId: 1, depth, ents: [], projs: [], zones: [], bounds };
  }
  function placeProp(field, prop, x, y) {
    const def = PROPS[prop];
    const e = { id: field.nextId++, type: "prop", prop, x, y, hp: def.hp, flash: 0 };
    field.ents.push(e);
    return e;
  }
  function spawnPickup(field, kind, x, y, ref, amount = 1, uses) {
    const a = rand(field) * Math.PI * 2, s = (40 + rand(field) * 70) * (kind === "weapon" ? 0.6 : 1);
    const p = {
      id: field.nextId++,
      type: "pickup",
      kind,
      ref,
      amount,
      uses,
      x,
      y,
      z: 30,
      vx: Math.cos(a) * s,
      vy: Math.sin(a) * s * 0.4,
      vz: 260 + rand(field) * 120,
      age: 0
    };
    field.ents.push(p);
    return p;
  }
  function rollDrops(field, run, table, x, y) {
    const rows = DROP_TABLES[table];
    const out = [];
    if (!rows) return out;
    for (const r of rows) {
      let p = r.p;
      if (r.kind === "ammo" && usesAmmo(run) && run.mag + run.reserve < 8) p = Math.min(1, p + 0.25);
      if (rand(field) >= p) continue;
      const kind = r.kind;
      if (kind === "ammo" && !usesAmmo(run)) continue;
      const ref = r.pool ? pick(field, r.pool) : void 0;
      spawnPickup(field, kind, x, y, ref, 1);
      out.push({ t: "drop", kind, ref, x, y });
    }
    return out;
  }
  var dropFromKill = rollDrops;
  function burst(field, kind, x, y, r, life, slow) {
    field.zones.push({ kind, x, y, r, t: 0, life, slow });
    return { t: "burst", kind, x, y, r };
  }
  function slowAt(field, x, y) {
    let m = 1;
    for (const z of field.zones) if (z.kind === "smoke" && Math.hypot(x - z.x, (y - z.y) * 2) < z.r) m = Math.min(m, 1 - (z.slow ?? 0));
    return m;
  }
  var near = (a, b) => Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, (a.y - b.y) * 1.6);
  function hitsTarget(p, t, pad = 0) {
    return Math.abs(p.x - t.x) < t.w / 2 + pad && Math.abs(p.y - t.y) < 34 + pad && p.z >= (t.z ?? 0) - 10 && p.z <= (t.z ?? 0) + t.h;
  }
  function propBox(e, p, pad = 0) {
    const d3 = PROPS[e.prop];
    return Math.abs(p.x - e.x) < d3.w / 2 + pad && Math.abs(p.y - e.y) < 30 + pad && p.z <= d3.h + pad;
  }
  function damageProp(field, run, e, dmg, targets = []) {
    if (!field.ents.includes(e)) return [];
    const def = PROPS[e.prop];
    if (def.use === "throw") return [];
    e.hp -= dmg;
    e.flash = 0.12;
    if (e.hp > 0) return [{ t: "propHit", prop: e.prop, x: e.x, y: e.y }];
    field.ents.splice(field.ents.indexOf(e), 1);
    const ev = [{ t: "break", prop: e.prop, x: e.x, y: e.y }];
    if (def.loot) ev.push(...rollDrops(field, run, def.loot, e.x, e.y));
    if (def.burst) ev.push(...explode(field, run, def.burst, e.x, e.y, def.id, targets, def.knockdown));
    return ev;
  }
  function explode(field, run, b, x, y, source, targets, knockdown) {
    const ev = [burst(field, b.type, x, y, b.radius, b.time ?? 0.5, b.slow)];
    if (b.damage) {
      for (const t of targets)
        if (Math.hypot(t.x - x, (t.y - y) * 2) < b.radius)
          ev.push({ t: "hit", target: t.id, damage: b.damage, knockback: 180, knockdown, status: b.type === "shock" ? "stun" : void 0, stun: b.type === "shock" ? 1 : void 0, x: t.x, y: t.y, source });
      for (const e of field.ents.slice())
        if (e.type === "prop" && Math.hypot(e.x - x, (e.y - y) * 2) < b.radius && field.ents.includes(e))
          ev.push(...damageProp(field, run, e, b.damage, targets));
    }
    return ev;
  }
  function attack(field, run, actor, targets, melee = { damage: 12, reach: 110 }) {
    const ev = [];
    if (run.carry) {
      const def = PROPS[run.carry.prop];
      field.projs.push({
        id: field.nextId++,
        kind: "prop",
        ref: def.id,
        x: actor.x + actor.dir * 30,
        y: actor.y,
        z: actor.z + 120,
        vx: actor.dir * 760,
        vz: 120,
        life: 1.6,
        damage: def.damage,
        knockback: 220,
        knockdown: def.knockdown,
        hit: []
      });
      ev.push({ t: "throw", prop: def.id });
      run.carry = null;
      return { events: ev, handled: true };
    }
    const w = useWeapon(run, "melee");
    const reach = w ? w.def.reach : melee.reach, dmg = w ? w.def.damage : melee.damage;
    const box2 = { x: actor.x + actor.dir * reach * 0.5, y: actor.y, z: actor.z + 80 };
    if (w) {
      ev.push({ t: "swing", weapon: w.def.id, left: w.left });
      for (const t of targets)
        if (Math.abs(t.x - box2.x) < reach * 0.5 + t.w / 2 && Math.abs(t.y - actor.y) < 40)
          ev.push({
            t: "hit",
            target: t.id,
            damage: dmg,
            knockback: w.def.knockback,
            knockdown: false,
            status: w.def.status,
            armorBreak: w.def.armorBreak,
            x: t.x,
            y: t.y,
            source: w.def.id
          });
      if (w.broke) ev.push({ t: "weaponBreak", weapon: w.def.id });
    }
    for (const e of field.ents.slice())
      if (e.type === "prop" && PROPS[e.prop].use !== "throw" && Math.abs(e.x - box2.x) < reach * 0.5 + PROPS[e.prop].w / 2 && Math.abs(e.y - actor.y) < 44)
        ev.push(...damageProp(field, run, e, dmg, targets));
    return { events: ev, handled: !!w };
  }
  function shoot(field, run, actor, held = false) {
    const ev = [];
    const g = run.held && WEAPONS[run.held.id].cls === "gun" ? WEAPONS[run.held.id] : null;
    if (g) {
      if (held && !g.rate) return ev;
      if (run.shotCd > 0) return ev;
      const w = useWeapon(run, "gun");
      run.shotCd = g.rate ?? 0.28;
      const n = g.pellets ?? 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnBullet(field, actor, g.damage, g.knockback, g.reach, g.id, n > 1 ? (i / (n - 1) - 0.5) * (g.spread ?? 0) : 0, g.vsHuman);
      ev.push({ t: "shot", source: g.id, x: actor.x, y: actor.y, dir: actor.dir });
      if (w.broke) ev.push({ t: "weaponBreak", weapon: g.id });
      return ev;
    }
    if (held) return ev;
    const r = fireSignature(run);
    if (r === "fire") {
      spawnBullet(field, actor, 10, 30, 640, "signature", 0);
      ev.push({ t: "shot", source: "signature", x: actor.x, y: actor.y, dir: actor.dir });
      if (run.reloadT > 0) ev.push({ t: "reload" });
    } else if (r === "dry") ev.push({ t: "dry" });
    else if (r === "reloading" && run.reloadT > 0 && run.shotCd === 0) {
      run.shotCd = 0.2;
      ev.push({ t: "reload" });
    }
    return ev;
  }
  function spawnBullet(field, a, damage, knockback, reach, ref, dy, vsHuman) {
    const speed = 1500;
    field.projs.push({
      id: field.nextId++,
      kind: "bullet",
      ref,
      x: a.x + a.dir * 50,
      y: a.y + dy,
      z: a.z + 130,
      vx: a.dir * speed,
      vz: 0,
      life: reach / speed,
      damage,
      knockback,
      knockdown: false,
      vsHuman,
      hit: []
    });
  }
  function interact(field, run, actor) {
    if (run.carry) {
      const e = placeProp(field, run.carry.prop, actor.x + actor.dir * 50, actor.y);
      e.hp = run.carry.hp;
      run.carry = null;
      return [];
    }
    let best = null, bd = PICKUP.interact;
    for (const e of field.ents) {
      const d3 = near(e, actor);
      if (d3 >= bd) continue;
      if (e.type === "pickup" && (e.kind === "weapon" || e.kind === "item") && e.z < 8) {
        best = e;
        bd = d3;
      } else if (e.type === "prop" && PROPS[e.prop].use !== "break" && !run.held) {
        best = e;
        bd = d3;
      }
    }
    if (!best) return [];
    if (best.type === "prop") {
      field.ents.splice(field.ents.indexOf(best), 1);
      run.carry = { prop: best.prop, hp: best.hp };
      return [{ t: "lift", prop: best.prop }];
    }
    return take(field, run, best, true);
  }
  function take(field, run, p, manual, ctx) {
    if (!field.ents.includes(p)) return [];
    const at2 = { x: p.x, y: p.y };
    let amount = p.amount;
    switch (p.kind) {
      case "heal":
        if (ctx) {
          if (ctx.hp >= ctx.max) return [];
          amount = Math.min(p.amount, Math.ceil((ctx.max - ctx.hp) / pickupHealAmount(ctx.max)));
          ctx.hp = Math.min(ctx.max, ctx.hp + pickupHealAmount(ctx.max, amount));
        }
        break;
      case "ammo": {
        const ammo = SIGNATURES[run.agent]?.ammo;
        if (!ammo || ammo === "none") return [];
        p.ammoLeft ??= AMMO_PICKUP[ammo].amount * p.amount;
        amount = addAmmo(run, p.ammoLeft);
        if (!amount) return manual ? [{ t: "full", kind: "ammo" }] : [];
        p.ammoLeft -= amount;
        if (!p.ammoLeft) field.ents.splice(field.ents.indexOf(p), 1);
        return [{ t: "pickup", kind: p.kind, amount, ...at2 }];
      }
      case "item":
        amount = addItem(run, p.ref, p.amount);
        if (!amount) return manual ? [{ t: "full", kind: "item", ref: p.ref }] : [];
        break;
      case "weapon": {
        const prev = equip(run, p.ref, p.uses);
        field.ents.splice(field.ents.indexOf(p), 1);
        if (prev) spawnPickup(field, "weapon", at2.x, at2.y, prev.id, 1, prev.uses);
        return [{ t: "equip", weapon: p.ref, dropped: prev?.id }];
      }
    }
    p.amount -= amount;
    if (p.amount <= 0) field.ents.splice(field.ents.indexOf(p), 1);
    return [{ t: "pickup", kind: p.kind, ref: p.ref, amount, ...at2 }];
  }
  function stepField(field, run, actor, input, targets, dt, ctx, melee) {
    const ev = [];
    ctx = ctx ? { ...ctx } : void 0;
    if (tickRun(run, dt)) ev.push({ t: "reloaded" });
    const b = field.bounds;
    for (const e of field.ents.slice()) {
      if (e.type === "prop") {
        e.flash = Math.max(0, e.flash - dt);
        continue;
      }
      e.age += dt;
      if (e.z > 0 || e.vz > 0) {
        e.vz -= 1400 * dt;
        e.z += e.vz * dt;
        e.x = Math.max(b.x0, Math.min(b.x1, e.x + e.vx * dt));
        e.y = Math.max(b.y0, Math.min(b.y1, e.y + e.vy * dt));
        if (e.z <= 0) {
          e.z = 0;
          e.vz = Math.abs(e.vz) > 200 ? -e.vz * 0.3 : 0;
          e.vx *= 0.5;
          e.vy *= 0.5;
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (e.age < 0.35 || actor.z > 40) continue;
      const auto = e.kind === "heal" || e.kind === "ammo";
      const d3 = near(e, actor);
      if (auto && d3 < PICKUP.magnet) {
        e.x += (actor.x - e.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        e.y += (actor.y - e.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
      }
      if (auto && d3 < PICKUP.touch || e.kind === "item" && d3 < PICKUP.touch) ev.push(...take(field, run, e, false, ctx));
    }
    if (input.interact) ev.push(...interact(field, run, actor));
    if (input.attack) ev.push(...attack(field, run, actor, targets, melee).events);
    if (input.shoot || input.shootHeld) ev.push(...shoot(field, run, actor, !input.shoot));
    if (input.use !== void 0) {
      const u = useItem(run, input.use, ctx);
      if (u) {
        ev.push({ t: "use", slot: input.use, id: u.id, effect: u.effect });
        const e = u.effect;
        if (e.type === "salt") ev.push(burst(field, "salt", actor.x, actor.y, e.radius, e.time));
        if (e.type === "flash" || e.type === "smoke")
          field.projs.push({
            id: field.nextId++,
            kind: "grenade",
            ref: u.id,
            x: actor.x + actor.dir * 30,
            y: actor.y,
            z: actor.z + 120,
            vx: actor.dir * 380,
            vz: 360,
            life: 3,
            damage: 0,
            knockback: 0,
            knockdown: false,
            hit: []
          });
      }
    }
    for (const p of field.projs.slice()) {
      p.life -= dt;
      p.x += p.vx * dt;
      if (p.kind !== "bullet") {
        p.vz -= 1300 * dt;
        p.z += p.vz * dt;
      }
      let done2 = p.life <= 0 || p.x < b.x0 - 60 || p.x > b.x1 + 60;
      if (p.kind === "grenade") {
        if (p.x < b.x0 || p.x > b.x1) {
          p.x = Math.max(b.x0, Math.min(b.x1, p.x));
          p.vx = 0;
        }
        done2 = p.life <= 0;
        if (p.z <= 0 || done2) {
          const def = ITEMS[p.ref].effect;
          if (def.type === "flash") {
            ev.push(burst(field, "flash", p.x, p.y, def.radius, 0.4));
            for (const t of targets)
              if (Math.hypot(t.x - p.x, (t.y - p.y) * 2) < def.radius)
                ev.push({ t: "hit", target: t.id, damage: 0, knockback: 0, knockdown: false, status: "stun", stun: def.stun, x: t.x, y: t.y, source: p.ref });
          } else if (def.type === "smoke") ev.push(burst(field, "smoke", p.x, p.y, def.radius, def.time, def.slow));
          done2 = true;
        }
      } else {
        for (const t of targets) {
          if (p.hit.includes(t.id) || !hitsTarget(p, t, p.kind === "prop" ? 16 : 0)) continue;
          p.hit.push(t.id);
          const dmg = Math.round(p.damage * (t.human && p.vsHuman ? p.vsHuman : 1));
          ev.push({ t: "hit", target: t.id, damage: dmg, knockback: p.knockback, knockdown: p.knockdown, status: p.status, armorBreak: p.armorBreak, x: t.x, y: t.y, source: p.ref });
          if (p.kind === "bullet" || p.kind === "prop") done2 = true;
          break;
        }
        if (!done2) {
          for (const e of field.ents)
            if (e.type === "prop" && propBox(e, p) && PROPS[e.prop].use !== "throw") {
              ev.push(...damageProp(field, run, e, Math.max(p.damage, 8), targets));
              done2 = true;
              break;
            }
        }
        if (p.kind === "prop" && (done2 || p.z <= 0)) {
          done2 = true;
          const def = PROPS[p.ref];
          ev.push({ t: "break", prop: p.ref, x: p.x, y: p.y });
          if (def.burst) ev.push(...explode(field, run, def.burst, p.x, p.y, def.id, targets, def.knockdown));
          else ev.push(burst(field, "blast", p.x, p.y, 40, 0.25));
        }
      }
      if (done2) field.projs.splice(field.projs.indexOf(p), 1);
    }
    for (const z of field.zones.slice()) {
      z.t += dt;
      if (z.t >= z.life) field.zones.splice(field.zones.indexOf(z), 1);
    }
    return ev;
  }

  // src/game/action/items/icons.ts
  var C2 = {
    heal: "#5fbf6a",
    cure: "#4fb3b3",
    throw: "#e08a3c",
    special: "#9a7fd1",
    metal: "#9aa4ab",
    dark: "#4a5258",
    wood: "#a07850",
    gold: "#d6b64a",
    cash: "#e6d36a",
    red: "#d65a5a",
    white: "#e8ecee",
    shard: "#c77dff"
  };
  var rect = (g, c, x, y, w, h) => {
    g.fillStyle = c;
    g.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
  };
  var circ = (g, c, x, y, r) => {
    g.fillStyle = c;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
  };
  var poly = (g, c, pts) => {
    g.fillStyle = c;
    g.beginPath();
    g.moveTo(pts[0], pts[1]);
    for (let i = 2; i < pts.length; i += 2) g.lineTo(pts[i], pts[i + 1]);
    g.closePath();
    g.fill();
  };
  var line = (g, c, w, pts) => {
    g.strokeStyle = c;
    g.lineWidth = w;
    g.lineCap = "round";
    g.beginPath();
    g.moveTo(pts[0], pts[1]);
    for (let i = 2; i < pts.length; i += 2) g.lineTo(pts[i], pts[i + 1]);
    g.stroke();
  };
  var bottle = (c, mark) => (g) => {
    rect(g, C2.dark, 13, 3, 6, 4);
    rect(g, c, 9, 7, 14, 21);
    rect(g, C2.white, 11, 13, 10, 8);
    rect(g, mark, 15, 14, 2, 6);
    rect(g, mark, 13, 16, 6, 2);
  };
  var ICONS2 = {
    medkit: (g) => {
      rect(g, C2.heal, 4, 9, 24, 18);
      rect(g, C2.dark, 12, 5, 8, 4);
      rect(g, C2.white, 14, 12, 4, 12);

  var thinImgs = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function drawItemIcon(ctx, id, x, y, size2) {
    const all = typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.__GOEI_ITEM_ART__ : void 0;
    const a = all?.[id];
    if (a && a.w > a.h * 2.2 && size2 <= 72 && a.src) {
      const src = a.src;
      let im = thinImgs.get(src);
      if (!im) thinImgs.set(src, im = Object.assign(new Image(), { src }));
      if (!im.complete || !im.naturalWidth) {
        im.addEventListener("load", () => drawItemIcon(ctx, id, x, y, size2), { once: true });
        return;
      }
      const k = size2 * 1.3 / a.w;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      ctx.translate(x + size2 / 2, y + size2 / 2);
      ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 4);
      ctx.drawImage(im, -a.w * k / 2, -a.h * k / 2, a.w * k, a.h * k);
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }
    if (drawItemArt(ctx, id, x, y, size2)) return;
    if (a) {
      const src = a.src;
      if (src) {
        const im = new Image();
        im.onload = () => drawItemIcon(ctx, id, x, y, size2);
        im.src = src;
      }
      return;
    }
    const d3 = ICONS2[id];
    if (!d3) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    ctx.scale(size2 / 32, size2 / 32);
    d3(ctx);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  // src/game/action/items/engine.ts
  var TICK = 30;
  var react = (knockdown, kb) => knockdown ? "down" : kb >= 140 ? "stagger" : "flinch";
  function itemHit(e, sourceX) {
    return {
      attackId: newAttackId(),
      x0: e.x,
      x1: e.x,
      z0: 0,
      z1: 0,
      y: e.y,
      dmg: e.damage,
      react: react(e.knockdown, e.knockback),
      dir: e.x < sourceX ? -1 : 1,
      push: e.knockback / TICK,
      armorBreak: e.armorBreak
    };
  }
  function toGameEvents(events, x, ammo = 0) {
    const out = [];
    for (const e of events) {
      switch (e.t) {
        case "pickup":
          out.push({ type: e.kind === "heal" ? "heal" : "pickup", x: e.x, id: e.ref ?? e.kind });
          break;
        case "equip":
          out.push({ type: "pickup", x, id: e.weapon });
          break;
        case "lift":
          out.push({ type: "pickup", x, id: e.prop });
          break;
        case "use":
          out.push({ type: e.effect.type === "heal" || e.effect.type === "cure" ? "heal" : "item", x, id: e.id });
          break;
        case "shot":
          out.push({ type: "shot", x: e.x, ammo });
          break;
        case "dry":
          out.push({ type: "empty", x });
          break;
        case "reload":
          out.push({ type: "reload", x });
          break;
        case "throw":
          out.push({ type: "whiff", x });
          break;
        case "break":
        case "burst":
        case "weaponBreak":
        case "full":
        case "drop":
          out.push({ type: "custom", name: "item:" + e.t, data: e });
          break;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  // src/game/action/items/link.ts
  var ENEMY_WEAPONS = {
    fb_smg: { p: 0.3, pool: ["smg"] },
    fb_sword: { p: 0.5, pool: ["katana"] },
    fb_chief: { p: 0.35, pool: ["servicepistol", "shotgun"] },
    fb_shield: { p: 0.2, pool: ["pipe"] },
    dd_member: { p: 0.1, pool: ["kitchenknife"] }
  };
  function spawnEnemyDrops(field, run, drops, x, y, kind = "") {
    const ev = [];
    const put2 = (k, ref, amount = 1) => {
      spawnPickup(field, k, x, y, ref, amount);
      ev.push({ t: "drop", kind: k, ref, x, y });
    };
    for (const d3 of drops) {
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(d3.n) || d3.n <= 0) continue;
      switch (d3.item) {
        case "heal":
          for (let i = 0; i < d3.n; i++) put2("heal");
          break;
        case "ammo":
          if (usesAmmo(run)) for (let i = 0; i < d3.n; i++) put2("ammo");
          break;
      }
    }
    if (usesAmmo(run) && run.mag + run.reserve < 8 && rand(field) < 0.25) put2("ammo");
    const w = ENEMY_WEAPONS[kind];
    if (w && rand(field) < w.p) put2("weapon", w.pool[Math.floor(rand(field) * w.pool.length)]);
    return ev;
  }
  function strikeProps(field, run, hits, seen2, targets = []) {
    const ev = [];
    if (!hits.length) return ev;
    for (const e of field.ents.slice()) {
      if (e.type !== "prop" || !field.ents.includes(e)) continue;
      const d3 = PROPS[e.prop];
      if (d3.use === "throw") continue;
      for (const h of hits) {
        const key = h.attackId + ":" + e.id;
        if (seen2.has(key)) continue;
        if (h.x1 < e.x - d3.w / 2 || h.x0 > e.x + d3.w / 2 || h.z0 > d3.h + 110 || h.z1 < 0 || Math.abs(h.y - e.y) > (h.dy ?? 18) + 24) continue;
        seen2.add(key);
        ev.push(...damageProp(field, run, e, Math.max(6, h.dmg), targets));
        break;
      }
    }
    if (seen2.size > 400) seen2.clear();
    return ev;
  }
  function furnishFloor(field, floor, lane, r = () => rand(field)) {
    const pick3 = (a) => a[Math.floor(r() * a.length)];
    const end = Number.isFinite(floor.exitX) ? floor.exitX : floor.length - 80;
    const x0 = (floor.startX ?? 200) + 260, x1 = Math.max(x0 + 200, Math.min(end, floor.length - 80) - 160);
    const taken = (floor.props ?? []).map((p) => [p.x - (p.w ?? 40) / 2 - 60, p.x + (p.w ?? 40) / 2 + 60]);
    const spot = () => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
        const x = x0 + r() * (x1 - x0);
        if (!taken.some(([a, b]) => x > a && x < b)) {
          taken.push([x - 90, x + 90]);
          return x;
        }
      }
      return null;
    };
    const front = lane[1] - 20, back = lane[0] + 10;
    const put2 = (id, y) => {
      const x = spot();
      if (x !== null) placeProp(field, id, x, y);
    };
    put2(pick3(["chair", "bin", "pot"]), front - r() * 40);
    put2(pick3(["tv", "extinguisher", "table", "chair"]), front - 20 - r() * 40);
    put2(pick3(["crate", "crate", "drawer", "mailbox"]), back);
    put2(pick3(["barrel", "locker", "crate"]), back + r() * 20);
    const wx = spot();
    if (wx !== null) {
      const p = spawnPickup(field, "weapon", wx, (lane[0] + lane[1]) / 2, pick3(Object.keys(WEAPONS).filter((id) => id !== "katana" && id !== "smg")));
      Object.assign(p, { vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, z: 0 });
    }
  }

  // src/game/action/enemy/geom.ts
  function overlap2(a, b) {
    return a.x0 < b.x1 && b.x0 < a.x1 && a.z0 < b.z1 && b.z0 < a.z1 && Math.abs(a.y - b.y) <= (a.dy ?? 18) + (b.dy ?? 18);
  }
  function toWorld2(b, x, y, z, dir) {
    const a = x + b.fx0 * dir, c = x + b.fx1 * dir;
    return { x0: Math.min(a, c), x1: Math.max(a, c), z0: z + b.z0, z1: z + b.z1, y, dy: b.dy, part: b.part };
  }

  // src/game/action/enemy/debuff.ts
  var DEBUFF_TEXT = {
    bleed: "출혈 · 피가 멎지 않는다",
    taint: "오염 · 숨이 탁해진다",
    headache: "두통 · 머리가 울린다",
    confuse: "혼란 · 방향이 뒤집힌다",
    fear: "공포 · 몸이 굳는다",
    slow: "느려졌다",
    silence: "목소리가 안 나온다"
  };
  var seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakMap();
  var armed = [];
  function state(w) {
    let s = seen.get(w);
    if (!s) seen.set(w, s = { tick: -1, hp: w.player.hp, said: {} });
    return s;
  }
  function emitDebuff(w, debuff, from) {
    const s = state(w);
    const quiet = w.tick - (s.said[debuff.kind] ?? -999) < 90;
    if (!quiet) s.said[debuff.kind] = w.tick;
    w.emit({ type: "custom", name: "debuff", data: { ...debuff, from, text: quiet ? void 0 : DEBUFF_TEXT[debuff.kind] } });
  }
  function armDebuff(hit2, debuff, from) {
    armed.push({ hit: hit2, debuff, from });
  }
  function checkDebuffs(w) {
    const s = state(w);
    if (s.tick === w.tick) return;
    s.tick = w.tick;
    const p = w.player;
    if (p.hp < s.hp) {
      for (const a of armed) {
        const h = a.hit;
        if (p.x >= h.x0 - 30 && p.x <= h.x1 + 30 && Math.abs(p.y - h.y) <= (h.dy ?? 20) + 20) {
          emitDebuff(w, a.debuff, a.from);
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    s.hp = p.hp;
    armed = [];
  }

  var Proj = class {
    constructor(def, name, x, y, dir, owner, attackBase, mult = 1) {
      this.def = def;
      this.owner = owner;
      this.mult = mult;
      this.kind = "proj:" + name;
      this.label = def.label;
      this.x = x;
      this.y = y;
      this.z = def.z;
      this.dir = dir;
      this.vx = def.speed * dir;
      this.vz = def.vz ?? 0;
      this.half = def.w / 2;
      this.attackId = attackBase;
    }
    id = newBodyId();
    team = "enemy";
    kind;
    x;
    y;
    z;
    dir;
    hp = 1;
    hpMax = 1;
    alive = true;
    stop = 0;
    label;
    vx;
    vz;
    t = 0;
    half;
    done = false;
    attackId;
    box() {
      const d3 = this.def;
      return { x0: this.x - this.half, x1: this.x + this.half, z0: this.z, z1: this.z + d3.h, y: this.y, dy: d3.low ? 16 : 20 };
    }
    frame() {
      const d3 = this.def;
      if (!this.alive || d3.harmless || d3.arm && this.t < d3.arm) return { hit: [], hurt: [] };
      const hit2 = {
        ...this.box(),
        attackId: this.attackId,
        dmg: Math.round(d3.dmg * this.mult),
        react: d3.react,
        dir: this.dir,
        push: d3.push ?? 2,
        stop: 2,
        low: d3.low,
        armorBreak: d3.unblockable,
        strength: d3.react === "down" ? 3 : d3.react === "stagger" ? 2 : 1
      };
      if (d3.debuff) {
        hit2.debuff = d3.debuff;
        armDebuff(hit2, d3.debuff, this.id);
      }
      return { hit: [hit2], hurt: [] };
    }
    step(world) {
      const d3 = this.def;
      checkDebuffs(world);
      if (this.done) return void (this.alive = false);
      this.t++;
      if (d3.every && this.t % d3.every === 0) this.attackId = newAttackId();
      this.x += this.vx;
      if (d3.gravity) {
        this.z += this.vz;
        this.vz -= d3.gravity;
        if (this.z <= 0) {
          this.z = 0;
          world.fx("dust", this.x, this.y, 0);
          world.shake(4, 2);
          this.alive = false;
        }
      }
      if (d3.grow) this.half += d3.grow;
      if (d3.homeY) {
        const dy = world.player.y - this.y;
        this.y += Math.max(-d3.homeY, Math.min(d3.homeY, dy));
      }
      if (this.t >= d3.life) this.alive = false;
      if (!d3.pierce && !d3.harmless && !(d3.arm && this.t < d3.arm)) {
        const p = world.player;
        if (p.alive && p.frame().hurt.some((h) => overlap2(this.box(), h))) this.done = true;
      }
    }
    applyHit() {
      return { kind: "immune" };
    }
    draw(ctx) {
      const d3 = this.def;
      const b = this.box();
      ctx.save();
      if (d3.arm && this.t < d3.arm) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25 + 0.5 * (this.t >> 2 & 1);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#ff4040";
        ctx.fillRect(b.x0, this.y - b.z1 + d3.h / 2 - 2, b.x1 - b.x0, 4);
        ctx.restore();
        return;
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = d3.harmless ? 0.55 * (1 - this.t / d3.life) + 0.2 : d3.pierce ? 0.55 : 0.95;
      ctx.fillStyle = d3.color;
      if (d3.grow) {
        ctx.fillRect(b.x0, this.y - b.z1, 10, b.z1 - b.z0);
        ctx.fillRect(b.x1 - 10, this.y - b.z1, 10, b.z1 - b.z0);
      } else ctx.fillRect(b.x0, this.y - b.z1, b.x1 - b.x0, b.z1 - b.z0);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
      ctx.fillStyle = "#000";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(this.x, this.y, Math.min(60, this.half), 6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  };

  // src/game/action/enemy/drops.ts
  var hook = null;
  function fireDropHook(c) {
    hook?.(c);
  }
  function rollDrops2(table, r, elite = false) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (item, n) => n > 0 && out.push({ item, n });
    const heal2 = (p) => r() < p + (elite ? 0.1 : 0) && add("heal", 1);
    switch (table) {
      case "none":
        return out;
      case "common":
        heal2(0.15);
        break;
      case "bureau":
        heal2(0.1);
        if (r() < 0.05) add("ammo", 1);
        break;
      case "daydream":
        heal2(0.1);
        break;
      case "mid":
        add("heal", 1);
        break;
      case "guardian":
        add("heal", 2);
        break;
    }
    return out;
  }

  function lookFor(kind) {
    return LOOKS[kind] ?? null;
  }
  function poseOf(e) {
    const f = e.frameNow();
    const p = { tilt: 0, lean: 0, step: 0, armF: 0.2, armB: -0.15, reach: 1, crouch: 0, lie: 0, open: 0 };
    switch (e.state) {
      case "walk":
      case "flee": {
        const s = Math.sin(e.age * (e.state === "flee" ? 0.5 : 0.32));
        p.step = s;
        p.armF = 0.15 - s * 0.5;
        p.armB = -0.15 + s * 0.5;
        p.lean = e.state === "flee" ? 0.25 : 0.04;
        p.crouch = Math.abs(s) * -3;
        break;
      }
      case "attack": {
        if (f?.tele) {
          p.lean = -0.16;
          p.armF = 2.5;
          p.armB = -0.6;
          p.crouch = 8;
          p.open = 0.5;
        } else if (f?.hit.length) {
          const b = f.hit[0];
          const shoulderZ = e.def.h * 0.78;
          const midZ = (b.z0 + b.z1) / 2;
          const midX = Math.max(10, (b.fx0 + b.fx1) / 2);
          p.armF = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(2.6, Math.atan2(midX, shoulderZ - midZ)));
          p.reach = Math.min(1.7, Math.max(1, Math.hypot(midX, shoulderZ - midZ) / (e.def.h * 0.42)));
          p.lean = 0.22;
          p.armB = -0.9;
          p.step = 0.7;
          p.open = 1;
        } else {
          p.lean = 0.1;
          p.armF = 1;
          p.step = 0.4;
        }
        break;
      }
      case "grabbing":
        p.armF = 1.5;
        p.armB = 1.3;
        p.lean = 0.15;
        p.open = 1;
        break;
      case "hurt":
      case "bound": {
        const k = Math.max(0, 1 - e.st / 10);
        p.tilt = -0.28 * (0.4 + 0.6 * k);
        p.lean = -0.18 * k;
        p.armF = 1.1;
        p.armB = 0.9;
        break;
      }
      case "stunned":
        p.tilt = Math.sin(e.age * 0.25) * 0.12;
        p.lean = 0.2;
        p.armF = 0.1;
        p.armB = 0.1;
        p.crouch = 10;
        break;
      case "air":
        p.tilt = -0.5 - Math.min(0.9, e.st * 0.05);
        p.armF = 2.2;
        p.armB = 2;
        p.step = 0.6;
        break;
      case "down":
      case "dead":
        p.lie = 1;
        p.armF = 2.9;
        p.armB = 3.05;
        break;
      case "getup":
        p.lie = Math.max(0, 1 - e.st / 12);
        p.crouch = 18 * p.lie;
        break;
      default:
        p.crouch = Math.sin(e.age * 0.09) * 2;
    }
    return p;
  }
  function lighten(c, k) {
    const m = c.startsWith("#") ? [parseInt(c.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(c.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(c.slice(5, 7), 16)] : (c.match(/\d+/g) ?? ["0", "0", "0"]).slice(0, 3).map(Number);
    return `rgb(${m.map((v) => Math.round(v + (255 - v) * k)).join(",")})`;
  }
  function dim(c, k = 0.7) {
    if (c === "#ffffff") return c;
    const m = c.startsWith("#") ? [parseInt(c.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(c.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(c.slice(5, 7), 16)] : (c.match(/\d+/g) ?? ["0", "0", "0"]).slice(0, 3).map(Number);
    return `rgb(${m.map((v) => Math.round(v * k)).join(",")})`;
  }
  function segEdge(ctx, w, x0, y0, x1, y1, bend, color) {
    if (color !== "#ffffff") seg(ctx, w + PIXEL * 1.6, x0, y0, x1, y1, bend, dim(color, 0.45));
    seg(ctx, w, x0, y0, x1, y1, bend, color);
  }
  function seg(ctx, w, x0, y0, x1, y1, bend, color) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
    ctx.lineWidth = w;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x0, y0);
    ctx.lineTo((x0 + x1) / 2 + bend, (y0 + y1) / 2);
    ctx.lineTo(x1, y1);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  function dot(ctx, x, y, r, color) {
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, Math.max(1, r), 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  function human(ctx, e, L, p, paint) {
    const h = e.def.h;
    const leg = h * (L.leg ?? 0.46) - p.crouch;
    const bulk = h * (L.bulk ?? 0.15);
    const r = h * (L.head ?? 0.075) * 1.2;
    const arm = h * (L.arm ?? 0.38);
    const hunch = h * (L.hunch ?? 0);
    const torso = h - leg - p.crouch - r * 2;
    const stride = h * 0.11;
    const hipY = -leg;
    const lean = p.lean + (L.hunch ?? 0) * 0.8;
    const neckX = Math.sin(lean) * torso + hunch * 0.4;
    const neckY = hipY - Math.cos(lean) * torso;
    const sx = neckX - Math.sin(lean) * bulk * 0.3;
    const sy = neckY + bulk * 0.35;
    const headX = neckX + Math.sin(lean) * r * 1.1 + r * 0.2;
    const headY = neckY - Math.cos(lean) * r * 0.95;
    const armEnd = (a, len) => [sx + Math.sin(a) * len, sy + Math.cos(a) * len];
    const [fx2, fy] = armEnd(p.armF, arm * p.reach);
    const [bx, by] = armEnd(p.armB, arm);
    const x = L.extras ?? [];
    const limbW = bulk * 0.5;
    const legW = bulk * 0.62;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    const legTo = (s, back) => {
      const footX = -s * stride;
      (back ? seg : segEdge)(ctx, legW, 0, hipY, footX, -legW * 0.3, h * 0.04, back ? dim(paint("bottom")) : paint("bottom"));
      seg(ctx, legW * 0.7, footX - legW * 0.1, -legW * 0.3, footX + legW * 0.9, -legW * 0.3, 0, paint("detail"));
    };
    seg(ctx, limbW, sx, sy, bx, by, -arm * 0.08, dim(paint("top")));
    dot(ctx, bx, by, limbW * 0.55, dim(paint("skin")));
    for (let i = 0; i < (L.arms ?? 0); i++) {
      const a = p.armB + 1 + i;
      seg(ctx, limbW * 0.8, sx, sy + 10 + i * 16, sx + Math.sin(a) * arm, sy + 10 + i * 16 + Math.cos(a) * arm, arm * 0.1, paint("skin"));
    }
    legTo(p.step, true);
    if (x.includes("backpack")) {
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("acc");
      const bw = h * 0.15;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.roundRect(neckX - bulk * 0.45 - bw, neckY + bulk * 0.15, bw, torso * 0.62, bw * 0.25);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("top");
    ctx.beginPath();
    const nx = Math.cos(lean), ny = Math.sin(lean);
    ctx.moveTo(neckX - nx * bulk * 0.55, neckY - ny * bulk * 0.55 + bulk * 0.15);
    ctx.lineTo(neckX + nx * bulk * 0.55, neckY + ny * bulk * 0.55 + bulk * 0.15);
    ctx.lineTo(nx * bulk * 0.42, hipY + ny * bulk * 0.42);
    ctx.lineTo(-nx * bulk * 0.42, hipY - ny * bulk * 0.42);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    dot(ctx, neckX, neckY + bulk * 0.2, bulk * 0.52, paint("top"));
    seg(ctx, legW * 1.15, -bulk * 0.05, hipY - 2, bulk * 0.05, hipY + legW * 0.4, 0, paint("bottom"));
    if (x.includes("coat") || x.includes("skirt") || x.includes("apron") || x.includes("melt")) {
      const low = x.includes("melt") ? 0 : x.includes("coat") ? -leg * 0.3 : -leg * 0.5;
      const flare = x.includes("melt") ? bulk * 1.5 : bulk * 0.8;
      ctx.fillStyle = x.includes("apron") ? paint("acc") : x.includes("coat") ? paint("top") : paint("bottom");
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-bulk * 0.48, hipY - bulk * 0.3);
      ctx.lineTo(bulk * 0.48, hipY - bulk * 0.3);
      ctx.lineTo(flare, low);
      ctx.lineTo(-flare, low);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
    }
    legTo(-p.step, false);
    if (x.includes("tie")) seg(ctx, bulk * 0.12, neckX + bulk * 0.3, neckY + bulk * 0.25, (neckX + 0) / 2 + bulk * 0.3, (neckY + hipY) / 2, 0, paint("acc"));
    if (x.includes("target")) {
      const tx = neckX / 2 - bulk * 0.4, ty = (hipY + neckY) / 2;
      dot(ctx, tx, ty, bulk * 0.36, paint("acc"));
      dot(ctx, tx, ty, bulk * 0.22, "#a83a2a");
      dot(ctx, tx, ty, bulk * 0.09, paint("acc"));
    }
    if (x.includes("strap")) seg(ctx, Math.max(3, bulk * 0.1), neckX - bulk * 0.35, neckY + bulk * 0.3, bulk * 0.4, hipY, 0, paint("acc"));
    if (x.includes("wrap"))
      for (let i = 1; i < 6; i++) {
        const t = i / 6;
        const yy = hipY + (neckY - hipY) * t;
        const xx = neckX * t;
        seg(ctx, 3, xx - bulk * 0.45, yy + 4, xx + bulk * 0.45, yy - 4, 0, "#6a5a40");
      }
    dot(ctx, neckX + r * 0.1, neckY + 2, r * 0.45, paint("skin"));
    dot(ctx, headX, headY, r, paint("skin"));
    if (x.includes("hair")) {
      dot(ctx, headX - r * 0.35, headY - r * 0.3, r * 1.1, paint("hair"));
      dot(ctx, headX + r * 0.1, headY - r * 0.55, r * 0.75, paint("hair"));
    } else if (L.hair) {
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("hair");
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(headX, headY, r * 1.04, Math.PI * 0.85, Math.PI * 1.9);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    if (x.includes("cap")) {
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("top");
      ctx.fillRect(headX - r * 1.02, headY - r * 1.15, r * 2.04, r * 0.75);
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("detail");
      ctx.fillRect(headX + r * 0.2, headY - r * 0.5, r * 1.3, r * 0.22);
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("acc");
      ctx.fillRect(headX - r * 0.1, headY - r * 0.95, r * 0.4, r * 0.3);
    }
    if (x.includes("mask")) {
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("skin");
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(headX - r * 0.6, headY - r * 0.6);
      ctx.lineTo(headX - r * 0.3, headY - r * 1.9);
      ctx.lineTo(headX + r * 0.15, headY - r * 0.8);
      ctx.fill();
      dot(ctx, headX + r * 0.9, headY + r * 0.2, r * 0.35, paint("skin"));
    }
    if (x.includes("glasses")) {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#05070a";
      ctx.fillRect(headX + r * 0.05, headY - r * 0.3, r * 0.98, r * 0.32);
    }
    if (x.includes("jaw") && p.open > 0) {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#4a0808";
      ctx.fillRect(headX + r * 0.15, headY + r * 0.15, r * 0.8, r * 1.5 * p.open);
    }
    segEdge(ctx, limbW, sx, sy, fx2, fy, arm * 0.06, paint("top"));
    dot(ctx, fx2, fy, limbW * 0.62, paint("skin"));
    if (x.includes("sword")) seg(ctx, Math.max(3, h * 0.016), fx2, fy, fx2 + Math.sin(p.armF + 0.4) * h * 0.42, fy + Math.cos(p.armF + 0.4) * h * 0.42, 0, paint("acc"));
    if (x.includes("shield")) {
      const shx = Math.max(fx2, sx + bulk * 0.6);
      ctx.fillStyle = paint("acc");
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.roundRect(shx - h * 0.02, sy - h * 0.14, h * 0.07, h * 0.52, 4);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  function spider(ctx, e, L, p, paint) {
    const big = e.kind === "legs";
    const w = e.def.w;
    const h = big ? e.def.h * 0.55 : e.def.h * 0.6;
    const cy = -(big ? e.def.h * 0.72 : h * 0.9);
    const legN = big ? 5 : 4;
    const walk = e.state === "walk" || e.state === "flee" ? e.age * 0.4 : 0;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      for (let i = 0; i < legN; i++) {
        const ph = Math.sin(walk + i * 1.3 + (side > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI)) * 8;
        const fx2 = side * (w * 0.25 + i * w * 0.07) + ph + (p.armF > 1.4 && side > 0 && i === legN - 1 ? w * 0.3 : 0);
        const kx = fx2 * 0.6;
        const ky = cy - (big ? e.def.h * 0.3 : h * 0.35);
        ctx.strokeStyle = side < 0 ? paint("detail") : paint("skin");
        ctx.lineWidth = big ? e.def.h * 0.045 : Math.max(5, h * 0.12);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, cy);
        ctx.lineTo(kx, ky);
        ctx.lineTo(fx2, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      if (side < 0) {
        ctx.fillStyle = paint("top");
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(-w * 0.06, cy, w * 0.22, h * 0.34, p.tilt, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) dot(ctx, -w * 0.18 + k * w * 0.07, cy - h * 0.22, h * 0.07, paint("skin"));
        dot(ctx, w * 0.16, cy - 4, Math.min(w * 0.11, h * 0.24), paint("skin"));
      }
    }
  }
  function beast(ctx, e, L, p, paint) {
    const w = e.def.w;
    const h = e.def.h;
    const sway = e.state === "walk" ? Math.sin(e.age * 0.2) * 6 : 0;
    const jaw = p.open * 0.5 + (e.weakOpen > 0 ? 0.6 : 0);
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    for (const lx of [-0.28, 0.06]) seg(ctx, h * 0.1, w * lx, -h * 0.35, w * lx - sway, 0, 6, paint("detail"));
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("top");
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(-w * 0.08, -h * 0.5 + sway * 0.3, w * 0.36, h * 0.3, -0.08, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("detail");
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const bx = -w * 0.34 + i * w * 0.09;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(bx, -h * 0.74);
      ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.03, -h * 0.9);
      ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.06, -h * 0.74);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    seg(ctx, h * 0.12, -w * 0.4, -h * 0.5, -w * 0.55, -h * 0.12 + sway, -w * 0.1, paint("top"));
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("skin");
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(w * 0.16, -h * 0.74);
    ctx.lineTo(w * 0.5, -h * 0.55 - jaw * h * 0.25);
    ctx.lineTo(w * 0.22, -h * 0.45);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.moveTo(w * 0.2, -h * 0.42);
    ctx.lineTo(w * 0.46, -h * 0.36 + jaw * h * 0.2);
    ctx.lineTo(w * 0.14, -h * 0.24);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("acc");
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const tx = w * (0.26 + i * 0.05);
      ctx.fillRect(tx, -h * 0.5 - jaw * h * 0.1 * i * 0.3, 3, h * 0.05);
    }
    for (const lx of [-0.12, 0.2]) seg(ctx, h * 0.11, w * lx, -h * 0.35, w * lx + sway, 0, 6, paint("top"));
  }
  function snake(ctx, e, L, p, paint) {
    const w = e.def.w;
    const h = e.def.h;
    const wave = e.age * 0.15;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("acc");
    ctx.fillRect(-w * 0.52, -h * 0.4, w * 0.27, h * 0.4);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7aa8b8";
    ctx.fillRect(-w * 0.49, -h * 0.35, w * 0.2, h * 0.26);
    ctx.strokeStyle = paint("top");
    ctx.lineWidth = h * 0.22;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-w * 0.38, -h * 0.2);
    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) {
      const t = i / 8;
      ctx.lineTo(-w * 0.38 + t * w * 0.68, -h * 0.12 - Math.sin(wave + t * 5) * 10 - t * t * h * 0.5 * (1 + p.lean));
    }
    ctx.stroke();
    dot(ctx, w * 0.32, -h * 0.68 * (1 + p.lean * 0.5), h * 0.14, paint("skin"));
    ctx.fillStyle = "#1c1a18";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(w * 0.3, -h * 0.68 * (1 + p.lean * 0.5), h * 0.15, Math.PI * 0.9, Math.PI * 1.9);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  function hand(ctx, e, p, paint) {
    const h = e.def.h;
    const reach = p.open ? 1.25 : 1;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.strokeStyle = paint("skin");
    ctx.lineWidth = h * 0.08;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-10, -h * 0.2);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(10, -h * 0.75, 30 * reach, -h * 0.85);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = h * 0.028;
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      ctx.moveTo(30 * reach, -h * 0.85);
      ctx.lineTo(30 * reach + h * 0.12 + i * 2, -h * 0.85 - h * 0.09 + i * h * 0.055);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  function furniture(ctx, e, L, p, paint) {
    const w = e.def.w;
    const h = e.def.h;
    const wig = Math.sin(e.age * 0.6) * 4;
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    for (const [lx, s, part] of [[-0.38, 1, "detail"], [0.15, 1, "detail"], [-0.15, -1, "top"], [0.38, -1, "top"]]) seg(ctx, Math.max(5, h * 0.1), w * lx, -h * 0.6, w * lx + wig * s, 0, wig, paint(part));
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("top");
    ctx.fillRect(-w * 0.47, -h * 0.78, w * 0.94, h * 0.2);
    ctx.fillStyle = paint("acc");
    ctx.fillRect(-w * 0.47, -h * 0.82, w * 0.94, h * 0.06);
  }
  function drawShape(ctx, e, L, p, paint) {
    if (L.shape === "human") human(ctx, e, L, p, paint);
    else if (L.shape === "spider") spider(ctx, e, L, p, paint);
    else if (L.shape === "beast") beast(ctx, e, L, p, paint);
    else if (L.shape === "snake") snake(ctx, e, L, p, paint);
    else if (L.shape === "hand") hand(ctx, e, p, paint);
    else furniture(ctx, e, L, p, paint);
  }
  var CW = 240;
  var CH = 280;
  var OX = 120;
  var OY = 140;
  var boards = null;
  function board(i) {
    if (!boards) {
      boards = [];
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
        const c = typeof OffscreenCanvas !== "undefined" ? new OffscreenCanvas(CW, CH) : Object.assign(document.createElement("canvas"), { width: CW, height: CH });
        boards.push({ c, x: c.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: k === 0 }) });
      }
    }
    const b = boards[i];
    b.x.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    b.x.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
    b.x.globalAlpha = 1;
    b.x.clearRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
    return b;
  }
  function mix(hex2, to, k) {
    const a = parseInt(hex2.slice(1), 16), b = parseInt(to.slice(1), 16);
    const ch = (s) => Math.round((a >> s & 255) * (1 - k) + (b >> s & 255) * k);
    return `rgb(${ch(16)},${ch(8)},${ch(0)})`;
  }

  var OUTFITS = [
    ["#8a3a34", "#2e2a2c", "#1c1612"],
    // 붉은 재킷
    ["#3f6a52", "#2a2f2c", "#141414"],
    // 녹색 점퍼
    ["#5c4f86", "#2a2836", "#3a2a1e"],

    ["#a08450", "#3d3426", "#5a4a3a"],

    ["#3c5a8a", "#22283a", "#101012"],
    // 파란 셔츠
    ["#7a5a3c", "#4a3c2c", "#24201c"],
    // 갈색 양복
    ["#c8b890", "#3a4458", "#2a2622"],
    // 베이지 니트
    ["#4a4a52", "#6a5a46", "#c8c4bc"]
    // 회색 정장, 흰머리
  ];
  function varied(e, L) {
    if (L.shape !== "human" || e.boss || e.kind.startsWith("fb_")) return { L, scale: 1 };
    const v = e.id * 2654435761 >>> 0;
    const o = OUTFITS[v % OUTFITS.length];
    const k = 0.75 + (v >> 4) % 3 * 0.12;
    return {
      L: {
        ...L,
        top: mix(L.top ?? "#5a5a5a", o[0], Math.min(1, k)),
        bottom: mix(L.bottom ?? "#2a2a2a", o[1], Math.min(1, k)),
        hair: (v >> 7) % 3 ? o[2] : L.hair,
        // 얼굴도 회색 가면처럼 보이지 않게 사람 피부색 몇 가지 (독립 검수: 회색 무표정)
        skin: mix(L.skin ?? "#a8927e", ["#c8a080", "#b08460", "#d4b090", "#9a7458"][(v >>> 14) % 4], 0.75),
        bulk: (L.bulk ?? 0.16) * (0.86 + (v >> 9) % 4 * 0.07)
      },
      scale: 0.92 + (v >> 12) % 4 * 0.04
    };
  }
  function drawFigure(e, ctx, outline) {
    const L0 = lookFor(e.kind);
    if (!L0 || typeof document === "undefined") return false;

    const p = poseOf(e);
    const base2 = e.def.color;
    const col = {
      top: L.top ?? mix(base2, "#101216", 0.35),
      bottom: L.bottom ?? mix(base2, "#101216", 0.6),
      skin: L.skin ?? mix(base2, "#c8c0b0", 0.3),
      acc: L.acc ?? mix(base2, "#e8e0d0", 0.4),
      hair: L.hair ?? "#1c1a1a",
      detail: "#14161a"
    };
    const flash = e.flash > 0 && p.lie === 0 && e.state !== "dead" && e.state !== "down";
    const transform = (x) => {
      x.setTransform(1 / PIXEL, 0, 0, 1 / PIXEL, OX, OY + (e.mem.cling && L.shape === "spider" ? -40 : 40));
      x.scale(e.dir * scale, scale);
      const upright = L.shape === "human" || L
